[2026-08-26]  v0.9.723: dungeon floor loot + egg sourcing — version + changelog
Bundles the dungeon-B slice: Azure Dreams floor-loot system (auto-pickup
items on dungeon floors, retiring the old treasure/scatter/hoard tiles;
gated to personal instances so world dungeons don't waste it), dungeon eggs
from boss + type-matched floor loot, and the overworld all-tier egg lottery.
Runtime unchanged (r1).

[2026-08-25]  v0.9.717: deck-screen fixes (loaner cards, permanence + mastery progress, tooltip)
Addresses playtest feedback on the new deck screen:
- Loaner companion cards (active while the companion is equipped, owned-copy
  count 0) now render LIT with a gold "LOAN" badge instead of greyed "OUT",
  and appear in the "Your Deck" strip (build_deck_card gains is_loaner).
- Loaner cards show permanence progress ("X/N casts to keep") via
  _loaner_permanence_text instead of the generic "use it to keep".
- Every owned deck card shows a mastery rank-progress line (R2 Adept
  (45/200)) so players see how close a card is to leveling without flipping.
- Removed the native Godot tooltip from the "Your Deck" strip tiles (the
  hover box we avoid); the catalog card below still flips for full details.

[2026-08-25]  v0.9.713: fix the GATHERING loot cards (the actual reported panel) — fixed size
Screenshot (finally read from the installed client's folder) confirmed the stretch/
overlap was the GATHERING scratch-off cards, not combat. _build_slot_card was a
PanelContainer that grows to its child's min size; the v0.9.710 clip_contents only
clipped the visual, not the layout size, so a tall reveal (kind badge + long/
multi-line name) grew the card and overlapped its jittered neighbours. Switched to a
fixed-size Panel + a full-rect anchored + clipped inner VBox so every card stays
exactly CARD_SIZE. (Combat _build_card got the same treatment earlier this build.)

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-08-25]  v0.9.713: first-strike in combat log + fixed-size loot cards
- Pre-Round-1 first strike missing from the log: when the monster wins initiative it
  attacks before Round 1, but the message only went to the encounter/main text, never
  combat_state.combat_log — so the log window showed nothing. Now appended to the log.
- Loot cards stretched/non-uniform: _build_card used fit_content + a MINIMUM size, so a
  tall reveal (long rare name + kind tag) grew the card and stretched its whole
  GridContainer row. Now fixed size (112x76) + clip_contents + non-growing label.

[2026-08-25]  v0.9.712: changelog + version
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-08-25]  v0.9.712: FIX flock engine carry (real bug) + combat shuffle overlay sizing
MOMENTUM CARRY (was genuinely broken, not display): process_combat_command calls
end_combat BEFORE returning to the flock store, and end_combat ALWAYS erases
active_combats (preserve_buffs only skips clearing character buffs). So the v0.9.711
_flock_engine_carry read get_active_combat() = {} → captured 0 every time (and the
`if ac == null` guard missed the empty-dict). Fix: combat_manager snapshots
momentum/combo/focus into last_combat_engines[peer] BEFORE the erase; server reads
combat_mgr.get_last_combat_engines(peer). Now Devastate/Outsmart/Meteor investment
actually carries into the next flock member.

COMBAT SHUFFLE OVERLAY (non-uniform sizes + overlap): _make_shuffle_overlay used a
PanelContainer + fit_content label that grew with long rare names. Switched to a
fixed-size Panel + clipped full-rect label (matching the gathering fix from v0.9.710).

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-08-25]  v0.9.711: changelog + version
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-08-25]  v0.9.711 fixes: shuffle overlap, flock momentum display, treasure chest stack
- Shuffle overlap (playtest: boxes overlapping mid-shuffle): the faster/more-overlap
  swaps let multiple tweens target the same overlay, so cards got stuck between
  positions. Rewrote both panels to apply all swaps to the arrangement FIRST, then
  glide each overlay ONCE to its final cell (one tween per node, staggered).
- Flock momentum not showing (carry looked broken): the capture WORKED, but the flock
  combat_start sent result.combat_state — snapshotted at start_combat with momentum=0.
  Now re-serialize combat_state via get_combat_display() after applying the carry, so
  the client shows the carried Momentum/Read/Focus at flock start.
- Treasure chest stack loss: _open_treasure_chest did remove_at() on a stacked
  (is_consumable→quantity) chest, nuking the whole stack. Now decrements quantity and
  only removes the slot when the last chest is opened.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

[2026-08-25]  v0.9.710: version bump
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-08-25]  v0.9.710: rare-cell reveal display + faster shuffle + box-jank fix (playtest)
Playtest feedback on the gathering rares:
- "Bonus Find shows one item / unlabeled": rare cells had NO reveal badge and
  Prospector's Bonus showed only the catch. Added kind badges (MOTHERLODE /
  WILDCARD / AUTO-SELL / BONUS FIND / AFFIX) + name display (Motherlode shows Nx,
  Bonus Find shows "catch +bonus"). The bonus material WAS awarded server-side; it
  just wasn't shown. ("already flipped over" = the affixed test tool's +2 reveals
  pre-revealed cells — expected.)
- "shuffle too slow / needs more movement": per-swap 1.6→1.0 base, clamp
  0.11-0.28 → 0.06-0.16, overlap 0.7→0.5 (both panels); gathering swap count
  3+tier(cap12) → 8+tier*2(cap22).
- "bonus boxes look janky / bigger": the preview OVERLAY was a PanelContainer +
  fit_content label that grew with long names. Switched to a fixed-size Panel +
  clipped full-rect label; shortened preview names (Motherlode/Wildcard/Auto-Sell/
  Bonus Find); single-line Prospector reveal; clip_contents on the card.

Playtest feedback ("a bit better"): preview underwhelmed, rewards felt junky/small,
lacked juice; + "marginally better rewards, marginally rarer panel".

[2026-08-21]  v0.9.662: loot-as-chance for gathering/crafting + flock ramp + launcher platform fix + victory-card fix
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-08-21]  combat: flock scratch-off ramp + victory-card robustness (v0.9.662)
Flock chains now ramp the scratch-off odds — each extra monster killed in the
chain adds COMBAT_SCRATCH_FLOCK_STEP (12%) to the roll, capped at 0.90. A long
flock now builds toward a near-guaranteed jackpot (user-requested). Uses the
existing _final_flock_kills; empowered elites still start from their 60% base.

Victory-card fix: the immediate (queue-empty) show path did a bare synchronous
show_victory_card with no force-visible / linger, unlike the drained-queue path.
On a quick kill that could land on a transient panel and fail to stick. The
always-on combat loot bag used to re-establish the scene and mask it; now that
loot is occasional (v0.9.662) the drop was exposed as "victory screen doesn't
always appear." The immediate path now mirrors the deferred path (force visible
+ extend linger + 1s beat).

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-08-20]  loot-as-chance for gathering + crafting (v0.9.662)
Ports the combat treat-rhythm to the two systems the user is least happy with.
The gathering/crafting scratch-off no longer fires on every action — it's an
occasional jackpot (~12.5% base, ~50% on a rich node / rare recipe). Most
actions resolve instantly.

Gathering (all 4 jobs, all entry points route through _start_scratch_off_gathering):
- Roll show-minigame per gather (skip_gather_minigame players always auto).
- Instant path: server auto-reveals up to `budget` slots (no timing misses,
  fair vs. clean manual play), awards via the existing completion pipeline, and
  sends scratch_off_complete{instant:true}. Client shows a compact one-liner in
  game_output instead of the panel (_show_instant_gather_summary).
- Minigame path: jackpot reveal budget (x2) so a proc is a real haul.
- "Lucky" node = gathering-node tier >= 4.

Crafting:
- The scratch-off is gated behind the same roll (only when the recipe isn't
  trivial and the player hasn't opted out). Otherwise auto-resolves via the
  existing auto-skip path at a FAIR score of 2 (was only 3-trivial / 1-opted;
  the common path now gets 2 = no stealth nerf).
- A craft minigame proc floors best_score at 2, so the rare treat is always at
  least as good as an ordinary craft + upside (score 3 + duplicate/refund/tool
  bonus slots).
- "Rare" craft = recipe difficulty >= 60.

Also lowers COMBAT_SCRATCH_BASE_CHANCE 0.22 -> 0.125 per user. All chances/
thresholds are named constants near the top of server.gd for easy tuning.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-05-28]  v0.9.650: Monster ASCII scale now rewrites the font_size in BBCode
v0.9.646's Control.scale approach for the combat monster ASCII didn't
actually change the rendered text size — the parent art_holder has
clip_contents=true so any visual scale-up just got clipped at the
original boundary. Player saw the highlight rect grow but the ASCII
stayed the same size.

Now the applier calls set_monster_art_user_scale(s) which stores the
multiplier and re-runs _refresh_monster(). That function rewrites the
[font_size=N] tag inside the stored BBCode (N → N * scale, clamped ≥ 2)
and re-sets the label text — so the ASCII genuinely re-renders at the
new font size.

[2026-05-28]  v0.9.647: UI scale fixes — persistent button + map sprite re-sync
Two follow-ups to v0.9.646 from playtest feedback:

1. World map applier now triggers _on_window_resized() instead of setting
   the font directly. The earlier path set the font once and the player
   sprite overlay didn't re-sync, so the marker drifted off the map cells.
   Per-element scale is now read INSIDE _on_window_resized and multiplied
   into the existing computation, so the value sticks across subsequent
   re-renders.

   next to the chat Send button. The hotkey was undiscoverable AND
   unreachable during combat (Settings menu isn't accessible mid-fight).
   The chat input row stays visible in every game state.

principle so this isn't repeated.

[2026-05-28]  v0.9.646: Click-to-resize UI scale system (per-element)
Adds a per-element UI scale layer on top of the existing 7-category bulk
knobs. Enter via Settings → UI Scale Settings → [F]; the overlay tints the
screen, hover any registered element to outline it, click to anchor a +/-
popup near it. Each adjustment persists to user://ui_scale_per_element.json
so reload-after-restart keeps the player's tuning.

V1 registers 5 elements covering the 1080p problems the user named:
- Monster ASCII (combat) — was clipped at bottom on FX screen
- Player card (combat) — was covering monster ASCII at low res
- Companion card (combat) — same overlap
- HP bars strip (combat)
- World map

Other panels can register their own scalable elements by calling
ui_scale_manager.register(group_id, control, applier, display_name) at any
time. Manager auto-applies the persisted scale on register so newly-built
panels match player preferences.

Also fixes a pre-existing bug in the UI Scale chat menu — KEY_4 was running
both monster_art -0.1 AND game_output +0.1 because the elif for KEY_5 was
missing. Split into separate branches.

[2026-05-28]  v0.9.644: Combat loot scratch-off — Chain, Mystery, Trap cells
Engagement-arc first batch (combat loot prototype). Three new special cells
each rolled at ~1/12 per filler slot, joining the existing +2 Reveals cell:

- Chain (cyan ⚡): auto-reveals 4 orthogonal neighbors for free on flip.
  Shockwave ring FX from origin + 60ms staggered cascade pops on neighbors.
- Mystery (green ✦): server pre-rolls an inflated payload (3x valor / essence
  / material + small egg chance). Hidden until flipped. Gold shimmer overlay.
- Trap (red ☠): -1 reveal penalty as punishment. Disguised as normal sealed
  card. Red flash + small panel shake on reveal.

All three reuse existing scratch SFX players (jackpot / egg_found / dud) so no
new audio assets needed. Chain neighbors that hit a trap or mystery still
trigger their own FX in the cascade.

Drop math: ~32% of filler slots become special (4 sequential 1/12 pre-checks).
Expect 3-5 specials per 16-slot bag. Tuning knob lives at the top of
_build_combat_loot_filler.

imprints vs all abilities for relevance." Audited every ability × imprint

v0.9.637 helper:

- haste (Arcane Surge): scale spell_damage_bonus AND double_cast_chance
- forcefield: scale shield_value
- distract: scale enemy_distracted percent
- sabotage: scale debuff_amount (str/def reduction)

  through them.

Analyze, Vanish, Perfect Heist, Forethought, Tactical Retreat) are pure
utility/CC/instant-win and have no scaling knob — silent skip is by

[2026-05-25]  v0.9.637: Buff-ability rank-up rework + corpse hint accuracy
Player report: "War Cry just got a rank up and it had a Damage option or
a crit chance option (from Wyvern). Do either of these actually do
anything for war cry?" Answer was no — both were silent no-ops. Plus a
related observation that the corpse compass hint was wandering, fixed
in parallel.

combat_manager.gd:
- New _apply_buff_value_modifiers helper applies the same scaling stack
  (rank multiplier + bonus_damage imprint) to BUFF VALUES. War Cry /
  Berserk / Iron Skin / Fortify / Rally now respect the rank-up +Damage
  choice and Predator's Mark imprint.
- _apply_imprint_riders_after_cast relaxed: cast-trigger traits
  (enemy_miss / stun / charm / mana_drain / absorb) now fire on buff
  ability casts. Damage-required traits (bonus_damage already folded;
  bleed/poison/lifesteal) still skip on 0-damage casts.
- Wyvern crit imprint on a buff ability now adds a crit_chance buff for
  4 rounds (+6%/stack) instead of being a no-op. Meaningful semantic
  match for "Hunter's Eye sees more crits during your pumped-up window."
- Per-attack crit roll now reads crit_chance buff value live instead of
  using the combat-start snapshot, so mid-combat crit buffs (imprint,
  potion, scroll) take effect on the next attack.

server.gd:
- _check_nearby_corpses now picks the NEAREST corpse instead of the first
  in iteration order. Range tightened 75 → 40 tiles. Added intensity word
  ("very close" / "nearby" / "distant") so the player can tell warmth.
  Player report: "pops up a good bit when you're not too close to a corpse
  then falls off when you get around the area it's supposed to be."

[2026-05-24]  v0.9.635: Ten-fix batch — crafting wildcard miscount, hotzone gather visibility, repair cost 4x cheaper, corpse spawn fallback, Space-on-victory, dungeon movement dismiss, rank `--`, resource bar flicker, salvage all visibility
Client:
- Crafting @-wildcard material miscount (e.g. Heart 32/3 but Missing Materials):
  client display was counting market-listed monster parts via `materials`
  (effective = pouch + listings) but server's can_craft uses `pouch_materials`
  (pouch only — can't consume from listings). New crafting_pouch_materials
  var on the client, _count_group_materials switches to it for @ keys.
- Space on Victory screen no longer triggers Rest (Pitfall #7 — _input
  intercept dismisses card, _process polled Space as still held and fired
  action-bar slot 0). Added set_meta("hotkey_0_pressed", true) in the dismiss
  path. Audit confirmed only the post-loot victory case was affected.
- Dungeon-victory movement also dismisses persisted victory card. Overworld
  path had this; dungeon path was missing.
- Top-bar Rank no longer flips to '--' between character_update messages.
  Only send_character_update injects projected_rank; the 31 other paths
  using raw to_dict() were wiping it. _set_character_data now preserves
  the previous value when the new payload omits the field.
- Resource bar flicker on movement (1-frame visible=true → visible=false
  race between update_resource_bars_overlay and the _process hard-hide).
  Function collapsed to a no-op.
- Salvage All result no longer vanishes (v0.9.634 routed text to game_output
  but inventory panel reappearing covers it). New salvage_result_shown
  pending state holds the panel hidden until Continue press.

- Repair Valor cost reduced 4x: wear% × level × 2.5 → wear% × level × 0.625.
  Lv50 fully-broken weapon: 12,500 → 3,125 valor.
- Corpse spawn fallback: if 20 attempts to find a valid (non-blocked,

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.632: REAL companion border fix — data-loss in active_companion
Root cause (not a rendering problem):

  character.gd::activate_hatched_companion builds a fresh dict for
  active_companion and never copied the border_tier field over.
  Inspect view reads from collected_companions (intact record) so
  the border rendered there. Mini overlay, combat scene panel, and
  every other surface that reads from active_companion saw
  border_tier=0 and silently skipped the render.

Five prior fix attempts (v0.9.629–631) all chased a visual-
subtlety / font-size hypothesis. The data never reached the
renderer on those surfaces. Five iterations wasted on the wrong
hypothesis because I didn't trace the data path. The user told me
3-4 times the screenshots showed clearly contrasted borders on
Inspect and nothing on the others — the symptom screamed 'data
path divergence' from the start.

Fix: one-line addition in the active_companion dict construction.

  "border_tier": int(companion.get("border_tier", 0)),

Server-affecting (character.gd runs server-side; the bug fires on
every activate_companion message). Existing players need to dismiss
+ re-activate their companion to refresh the data, though the
load-time backfill at character.gd:1867 will re-roll a value on
their next session.

The 6-char edge width from v0.9.631 stays — cleaner read at small
fonts now that it actually applies everywhere.

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.631: Border edge width 2→6 chars (debug probe)
Per user direction. My v0.9.629/630 hypothesis was that the border

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.630: Release confirm + hatched border_tier + BBCode safety
Three companion fixes.

1. Release companion has a 2-stage confirmation now. Player report:
   misclicks could permanently delete a companion. First stage shows
   a full explanation (what release does, no resources returned,
   Sanctuary-registered ones unaffected) + Continue button. Second
   stage is FINAL CONFIRM. Mirrors the existing Release All flow.

   New state "release_final" between "release_confirm" (now
   warn-stage) and the server send.

2. Hatched companions inherit border_tier from their egg. Player
   report: "a lot of my companions don't seem to have a border."
   Root cause: both character.gd::_hatch_egg and the server's
   home-stone hatch were building the new companion dict without
   copying border_tier from the egg. Egg had it (rolled at create)
   but companion didn't → display fell back to Tier 0 = no border.
   Fixed both paths to forward egg.get("border_tier", 0).

3. v0.9.629 regression fix. The 2-char bold edge color broke
   pattern-recolored art (Ringed Zombie Thrall and others using
   gradient / striped / diagonal patterns). Their lines have inline
   [color=X]...[/color] tags per line; the edge wrap interpreted
   the `[` from the tag as the first non-ws char and wrapped `[c`
   in [b][color=...]...[/color][/b] — corrupting the BBCode. Player
   saw literal `olor=red]X[/color]` text on Inspect.

   Fix: apply_variant_border skips lines with inline tags
   (specifically: line has >=2 occurrences of either [color or
   [/color, or has both). Those keep their pattern color without
   the border-tier highlight. Trade-off accepted: pattern color
   already carries visual identity.

shared/character.gd, server/server.gd. Server-affecting (hatch
path lives on server).

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.626: Clear deferred victory payload on loot close / dismiss
Real root cause for the stuck combat scene + Review Damage button
after fast Space/movement during loot reveal:

_drain_combat_queue's queue-empty branch (line 31610) consumes
_pending_victory_card_payload by calling show_victory_card. The
payload was set at combat_end when the combat message queue was
non-empty (v0.9.413 deferred-display pattern: pace the attacks
before the card covers the panel).

If the queue is STILL DRAINING when the player dismisses the
victory card via Space (v0.9.612 intercept) or movement (v0.9.604
post-loot path), the queue-empty branch fires LATER and re-opens
the dismissed card. _victory_interlude_active flips back to true.
Panel sticks visible with Review Damage button up.

The previous v0.9.622-625 fixes (hide_fx_overlay_only, safety net
relaxation, explicit cleanup paths) all ran BEFORE the deferred
re-show fired, so they didn't prevent it.

Fix: nuke _pending_victory_card_payload + _pending_victory_fx_play
in three sites:
1. _on_combat_loot_closed — _combat_loot_refresh_victory_card
   already shows the card, the pending payload is redundant.
2. Space-dismiss intercept (_post_loot_victory_persists path).
3. Movement-dismiss path.

With these flags cleared, _drain_combat_queue's empty branch
becomes a no-op for those conditions and can't re-open the
dismissed card.

Apologies for the multi-version chase. The actual bug was the
deferred-payload pattern racing with the dismissal paths, not the
FX overlay state itself.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd. Client-only.

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.623: hide_fx_overlay_only — stop pre-FX layout leaking through
v0.9.622 fixed the stuck FX scene but introduced a regression: the

- Restores strips (no tween, just visible=true)
- Defers _update_review_button_visibility

Does NOT touch _player_col modulate. The pre-FX layout stays hidden
where the action phase left it. reset_for_new_combat handles the
full restore when a fresh combat starts.

_on_combat_loot_closed now calls hide_fx_overlay_only instead of
_force_end_action_phase.

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.622: FX overlay torn down on loot panel close
Bug: "Now a fight with a Gargoyle is stuck on my screen and not
clearing. ... It seems like maybe I moved or pressed space quickly
and it stayed up."

Root cause: the FX battlefield overlay is set visible during combat's
action phases. end_action_phase enters persistent-FX mode (overlay
stays visible for the rest of combat, no auto-fade — v0.9.593
design). The loot panel layers over this. When the loot panel
closes and the victory card eventually dismisses via Space or
movement (my v0.9.604 / v0.9.612 paths), the FX scene is exposed
underneath with nothing to clear it.

Fix: _on_combat_loot_closed now calls
combat_scene_panel._force_end_action_phase() before refreshing the
victory card. This tweens the overlay to alpha=0 + sets
visible=false on tween completion (the same teardown that
acknowledge_continue uses). The Review FX button still works —
start_review_phase calls start_action_phase which rebuilds the
overlay from scratch.

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.620: Bulk-list rune fix + over-leveled encounter scaling
Two player-reported fixes.

1. Bulk Equipment badge stuck at "(1)" after listing all equipment.
   Root cause: client count predicate was `entry.has("slot") or
   entry.has("rarity")` — too broad. Runes carry a rarity field
   (have tiers), so they hit the fallback and counted as equipment.
   Server's bulk-equipment filter explicitly EXCLUDES rune
   (server.gd:16883), so bulk-listing left runes in inventory and
   the badge stayed at "(N runes)". Fix: skip `type == "rune"`
   BEFORE the equipment check, mirroring the server filter.

2. Random encounter rate scales by player_level vs area_level.
   New check_encounter() signature: (x, y, player_level=0,
   bypass_level_scaling=false). When player_level > area_level,
   rate scales: rate *= clamp(1.0 - level_diff * 0.05, 0.1, 1.0).
   - At +5 levels above area: 75% rate
   - At +10: 50%
   - At +18+: 10% (floor)
   Hunt action keeps its own roll (server.gd:5233, unchanged) so
   over-leveled players can still farm low areas intentionally.
   Threat-corridor tiles bypass scaling — dungeon spillover monsters
   still pull players into danger zones regardless of level gap.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, client/market_panel.gd,
shared/world_system.gd, server/server.gd. Server-affecting.

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.619: Review Damage button shows during victory interlude
Fix: button was hidden during the entire victory card window.

Root cause: _drain_combat_queue's queue-empty branch fires
show_victory_card BEFORE it schedules end_action_phase_after(grace),
so at victory-card-display time _action_phase_active is still TRUE
— and _update_review_button_visibility hard-hides the button while
action phase is active. Then end_action_phase runs, persistent FX
kicks in, explicitly hides the button again. The button only ever
became visible AFTER _force_end_action_phase ran from
acknowledge_continue — by then the player has dismissed the card.

Fix: when _victory_interlude_active is true, override the
_action_phase_active gate. If the victory card is up we WANT the
button visible regardless of stale action-phase state.

The original behavior (hide during rounds, hide in review) is
preserved for all non-victory states.

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.618: Pull-down level math — permanent fix
Deep-dive memo (project_level_math_redesign.md) diagnosed why 5
iterations across v0.9.595/614/615/616/617 all had tradeoffs:

  The procedural network has 60 posts, not the ~18 the legacy
  commit comments assumed. All "far-post weight is negligible"
  math was calibrated for the wrong scenario. The pathological
  case isn't "near vs far" — it's "near vs slightly-less-near
  with a much higher anchor." At the user's worst spot (-39,-58)
  in a Lv 17 wild zone, the nearest post sat 52 tiles away with
  anchor 38 and the second-nearest 70 tiles away with anchor 1;
  the 1/d⁴ weight ratio was only 3.3:1, dragging the blend to 32.

Fix — Candidate B (pull-down blend):

  Posts contribute ONLY when their anchor is BELOW the wilderness
  curve. Bounded influence radius R=140 with smoothstep taper
  (TAPER=30) at the cutoff. Saturating strength so close-to-post
  tiles pull all the way to the weighted anchor.

Properties (verified in simulation across 3 synthetic 60-post layouts):

- Cannot elevate by construction. The anchor < wild gate is
  structural; no tuning value of R, TAPER, or weight power can
  reproduce the v0.9.614/615/616 elevation bug class.
- Continuity: max single-step jump = 1 across simulated probes
  (vs v0.9.595's 73, v0.9.615's 41, v0.9.617's reverted-to-595
  cliff).
- Apex (dist > 600): unchanged. No posts in range → returns
  wilderness directly.
- Inside player-post bubbles: unchanged. Bubble override still
  wins at the top of the function.

Effects:
- (-39,-58) drops 32 → 16 (matches wilderness floor)
- Mid-band ring (200-400) drops 7-12% near posts (feature:
  settlements feel slightly safer than open wilderness)
- Starter ring (30-150) drops mean 1 level (barely perceptible)
- ZERO tiles go UP

Also: multi-companion architecture memo locked direction during
this session (project_multi_companion_design.md):
- Cap source: Sanctuary house upgrade (Cohesion tier)
- HP model: individual KO per companion
- XP split: full 10% each
- No mid-combat swap in v1
- Imprint: all N traits stack with 4×N stack cap
- Party combat: capped to N=1 in v1
- Passive stacking: diminishing returns
- Layout: side-by-side blocks, ASCII aspect preserved
- Map sprite: all N trail snake-pattern
- Permadeath: Sanctuary protects full registered set

Phase 1 (back-compat data refactor) ready to start on greenlight.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, shared/world_system.gd.
Server-affecting.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-05-22]  v0.9.617: Hold — revert to v0.9.595 smoothstep while deep-dive lands
v0.9.616's all-posts 1/d⁴ IDW elevated baseline by ~10 levels —

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-05-22]  v0.9.616: All-posts 1/d^4 IDW — continuous everywhere, no swap cliffs
v0.9.615's top-3 1/d² had residual cliffs. Player report: (45,-57)
shows Lv 26 vs (44,-57) at Lv 18 — an 8-level jump where there
should be ~1 level smooth transition.

Diagnosis: top-N has a discrete SWAP cliff whenever the swap-slot's
post has a different anchor. At slot weight ~0.000156 (3rd-nearest)
and a 35-level anchor gap, the swap produces an 8-level blend
discontinuity. My v0.9.615 analysis ("sub-1 level swap") assumed
similar anchors at the swap boundary — wrong assumption.

Fix: drop discrete set membership entirely. Use ALL posts with
steeper 1/(d^4 + 1) weighting:

  level = Σ(w_i × anchor_i) / Σ(w_i),  w_i = 1/(d_i^4 + 1.0)

Properties:
- Every post always contributes (no set membership) → smooth weight
  redistribution as the player moves → no swap cliffs.
- 1/d^4 means a post 10× farther has 1/10000th the weight (vs 1/100th
  for p=2). At typical post spacing, 50 far posts cumulatively
  contribute <0.2% of the nearest's weight. v0.9.614's catastrophic
  +70 elevation cannot recur because the falloff is much steeper.
- Sparse maps + dense maps both work — same math, weight ratios
  scale with whatever post configuration exists.

Math at (44,-57): nearest at d≈15 contributes weight ~2e-5; entire
rest of network contributes ~3% of nearest. Blend stays close to
nearest's anchor (Lv 18) regardless of what's elsewhere.

At adjacent tile (45,-57): every post's weight shifts by tiny
amounts proportional to dr/r⁴ — the blend changes by <0.1 level
typically. The 4th-becoming-3rd case is now a smooth weight handoff
instead of a discrete identity swap.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, shared/world_system.gd.
Server-affecting.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-05-22]  v0.9.615: Top-3 nearest IDW (hotfix for v0.9.614's level inflation)
EMERGENCY hotfix for v0.9.614, which elevated starter-zone monster
levels from ~18 to ~89 across the entire post network. Player report:
"When I logged onto my barbarian the monsters were lvl 89ish in that
area now."

Root cause: v0.9.614 used 1/d² weighting across ALL posts in the
calc. The per-post intuition "far posts contribute negligibly"
held individually (1/100² ≈ 0.0001 vs 1/10² ≈ 0.01) but the
CUMULATIVE contribution of 50 distant high-anchor posts outweighed
the single nearest low-anchor post — pulling the blend UP toward
the apex-zone average.

Fix: cap the IDW calc set to the 3 NEAREST posts.

  level = Σ(w_i × anchor_i) / Σ(w_i),  w_i = 1/(d_i² + 1.0),
  i ∈ {top 3 nearest posts by d²}

Properties:
- Cumulative far-post elevation is mathematically impossible — only
  3 posts contribute regardless of how many exist on the map. Holds
  for any procedural map regeneration (~18 posts in starter network,
  expandable without breaking the math).
- Nearest still dominates: at typical post spacing the nearest has
  weight ~100× the 3rd-nearest, so blend stays close to nearest's
  anchor in normal play.
- Post-swap cliff (the (44,-57) → (45,-57) bug that motivated v0.9.614)
  is solved differently: the SWAP happens at the lowest-weight slot
  (the 3rd-nearest), so the discontinuity is sub-1 level vs the
  5-15 level cliff that smoothstep produced.
- Sparse-map safe: if fewer than 3 posts exist, top.size() ends up
  smaller; total_weight check handles 0/1/2 cases gracefully.

Apologies for the v0.9.614 danger window. Players in active threat
corridors may have encountered Lv 80-90 mobs during the brief deploy.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, shared/world_system.gd.
Server-affecting.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.614: Inverse-distance level blend kills second-nearest-swap cliff
Player report kept happening even after v0.9.605's bubble fix:
"(44,-57) is Lv ~18, moving one east to (45,-57) is Lv ~38."
The bubble falloff in v0.9.605 only smooths bubble edges — this is
a different cliff. The "second-nearest-post-swap" residual
documented in project_monster_level_smoothing: when the player
crosses a midpoint where the SECOND-nearest post identity flips,
the smoothstep blend recomputes against a completely different
anchor and the level jumps by |new_second - old_second| × weight.

Fix: replaced nearest+second-nearest pair selection with inverse-
distance² weighted blend across ALL nearby NPC posts.

  level = Σ(w_i × anchor_i) / Σ(w_i)
  w_i = 1 / (d_i² + 1.0)

Far posts contribute almost nothing (1/100² ≈ 0.0001 vs 1/10² ≈
0.01 — 100× less influence per 10× distance), nearest post still
dominates its own pocket. As the player moves, every post's weight
shifts smoothly. No post enters or leaves the calculation set, so
no identity-swap discontinuity.

ε = 1.0 in the denominator prevents singularity at exact post
coordinates (player standing on a post gets that post's anchor
level, not infinity).

Bubble layer (v0.9.605 falloff) unchanged — still runs on top of
the IDW blend. Safe zones inside bubbles work the same.

Perf: O(N) for nearest+second find → O(N) for full weighted sum.
Same complexity. Post count bounded (~50 in starter network).

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, shared/world_system.gd.
Server-affecting (world_system runs server-side).

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.613: Flock log archive captures fight content (drain-timing fix)
Bug fix: per-fight combat logs in flock chains were missing the
actual attacks and damage. The [L] view and Review FX strips only
showed end-of-combat / next-fight intro messages.

Root cause: v0.9.610 fired clear_log(true) inline in combat_end's
flock_incoming branch, but combat messages drain at a paced rhythm
AFTER combat_end arrives. The archive captured an EMPTY _log_lines
for the just-finished fight; the actual damage / ability messages
then drained into _log_lines and were swept up into the NEXT fight's
archive. Player report: "some [fights] don't show anything in the
combat strips aside from Tactical Discipline: -20% cost!"

Fix: archive is now DEFERRED to queue-drain.
- combat_end sets new flag _pending_flock_archive_request = true
- _drain_combat_queue's queue-empty branch (where the final messages
  have just landed) consumes the flag, calls clear_log(true), and
  emits the "But wait... you hear more Xs approaching" chrome AFTER
  the archive. Those lines now land in the NEXT fight's intro
  instead of buried at the tail of the archived fight.

Effect: each archived flock fight now contains its full sequence —
Round dividers, every attack (player + companion + monster), ability
casts, status effects, defeat / XP / passive chrome. [L] and Review
FX surface the complete play-by-play per fight.

Note: separate investigation confirmed the (44,-57) → (45,-57)
level cliff player reported earlier is the second-nearest-post-swap
residual documented in project_monster_level_smoothing — v0.9.605's
bubble falloff doesn't address it. Will be tackled in v0.9.614 with
inverse-distance weighting across 3+ nearest posts.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd. Client-only.

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.606: Phase A of +abilities arc — +X to damage abilities
Round 1 of the multi-release gear-abilities arc. Damage abilities can
now be powered up via gear that rolls '+X to <ability>' or '+X to
<archetype> damage abilities'. Each +1 lifts the ability's effective
mastery rank.

Per user direction: gear-granted ranks are NOT capped at the table
max — every rank past 6 adds +10% damage indefinitely. This is the
chase-power direction.

New affixes (added to CHASE_SUFFIX_POOL):

Specific damage abilities (+1 to +3 by item level):
- ability_rank_cleave  ("of Cleaving")
- ability_rank_power_strike  ("of Striking")
- ability_rank_shield_bash  ("of Bashing")
- ability_rank_devastate  ("of Crushing")
- ability_rank_magic_bolt  ("of Bolting")
- ability_rank_blast  ("of the Blast")
- ability_rank_meteor  ("of the Comet")
- ability_rank_ambush  ("of Stalking")
- ability_rank_exploit  ("of Plunder")

Archetype-wide (+1 to +2 by item level):
- ability_rank_warrior_dmg  ("of the Warrior")
- ability_rank_mage_dmg  ("of the Mage")
- ability_rank_trickster_dmg  ("of the Trickster")

Character:
- get_ability_rank_bonus(ability_name): sums affixes across slots,
  reading specific + archetype keys. Damage-only ability lists added
  (_WARRIOR_DAMAGE_ABILITIES / _MAGE_DAMAGE_ABILITIES /
  _TRICKSTER_DAMAGE_ABILITIES). Wear-affected.
- get_ability_damage_mult: now adds gear_bonus to base_rank before
  the multiplier lookup. New static helper _compute_rank_multiplier
  handles in-table lookup and +10% per rank past the array max.

Tooltip:
- New _format_chase_affix_lines helper covers both v0.9.599 chase
  stat affixes (damage_mult / crit_chance / hp_on_kill / etc.) AND
  the v0.9.606 ability_rank affixes. Each chase affix gets its own
  gold-tinted "Special:" line in the item tooltip.

Roadmap (deferred to follow-up releases):
- Phase B: per-ability rank-up scaling for non-damage abilities
  (the rank-up popup's "+X% Damage" pick is meaningless on War Cry,
  Iron Skin, Vanish, etc.). Each ability type gets an appropriate
  rank-up effect.
- Phase C: extend +X gear and imprints to non-damage abilities once
  Phase B's per-ability scaling lands.

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.605: Smooth bubble edge cliff
Player report: "at 44,-57 area is Lv ~18, moving one east to 45,-57
area is Lv ~38." A 20-level cliff in one tile.

Root cause: v0.9.595's smoothstep blend fixed post-to-post midpoint
cliffs, but player-post settler bubbles still had a hard edge at
their radius. Inside used level_for_tier(tier) (discrete), outside
fell through to the smooth post-anchored blend — and the boundary
snapped between two unrelated values.

Fix: replaced _bubble_level_at with _bubble_influence_at which
returns {level, weight}. Weight is 1.0 deep inside the bubble,
smoothstep falloff to 0.0 over BUBBLE_FALLOFF_TILES (8) tiles past
the radius. get_post_anchored_level uses the weight to lerp from
the world level toward the bubble level in the falloff band, so the
20-level cliff becomes a 20-level ramp over 8 tiles (~2.5 levels
per step). Safe-zone inner radius unchanged.

Legacy _bubble_level_at kept as a thin wrapper that returns the
discrete level only when weight >= 1.0 — covers any external caller
that ignored the falloff.

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.602: Combat-loot polish — 5 bugs + QWERTY scratchoff nav + PICKED marker
Player feedback batch — seven combat-loot / dungeon fixes.

1. Victory screen no longer flickers during loot reveal. Was redrawing
   the victory card behind the scratch-off on every reveal. Now the
   loot lines accumulate silently and the card refreshes ONCE atomically
   when the cascade finishes (in _on_combat_loot_closed).

2. Players can move freely after the loot panel closes. The
   _on_combat_loot_closed handler now clears pending_continue so the
   player doesn't have to press Space — the cascade-complete event IS
   the acknowledgement.

3. Dungeon healing tiles (Wyvern Down + 8 others) AND every other
   character-mutating dungeon tile (damage, resource regen, poison
   ticks, pending_* status metas like sacred_buff / war_banner /
   spectral_veil) now update the client. server.gd::handle_dungeon_move
   was missing the send_character_update call that handle_move has;
   one-line blanket fix at the end of the function.

4. Space no longer opens inventory in dungeons during loot reveal.
   v0.9.596 added a loot-reveal gate to the "flock" action_type only;
   in dungeons slot 0 binds to "Items" (action_type "local") so Space
   still leaked through. Gate moved to the top of trigger_action so
   it blocks every action_type while the loot panel is up.

5. Focus-color trail bug. Arrow-navigating across the loot grid used
   to leave the yellow focus border on every card you passed through.
   _apply_focus_visuals reset border WIDTH but not COLOR. Now
   re-renders the natural stylebox (sealed purple / rarity / dim
   missed) for non-focused cards before overlaying the focus border
   on the current target.

6. QWERTY-grid loot selection. Each of the 16 loot tiles is now bound
   to a direct single keypress in a positional layout:
     1 2 3 4    ←  top row
     Q W E R
     A S D F
     Z X C V    ←  bottom row
   Press the key and that tile reveals immediately. Arrow keys + Enter
   still work as fallback. Gathering scratch-off keeps arrow nav this
   release — jittered slot positions need an on-card key overlay,
   deferred to follow-up.

7. PICKED marker. Player-revealed loot tiles get a gold ★ prefix and
   3px border (vs 2px for missed). Cascade auto-reveals stay dimmed
   with the existing italic "(missed)" tag. Picked vs missed is
   unambiguous at a glance now.

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.600: Combat buffs carry through flock encounters
Player feedback: ability buffs (War Cry, Berserk, Iron Skin, Fortify,
etc.) should persist across the entire flock encounter instead of
resetting between each mob.

Root cause: combat_manager.end_combat was called inside
process_ability_command / process_attack the moment a monster died.
That call cleared character.active_buffs BEFORE the server got a
chance to roll the flock chance and queue the next mob. By the time
the second monster spawned, every buff was gone.

Fix: end_combat now takes a preserve_buffs flag (default false for
back-compat with all existing call sites). Combat manager sets it
true whenever the victory result has flock_chance > 0 OR
summon_next_fight set (summoner abilities also chain combats).
Server.gd clears the preserved buffs in the non-flock-victory branch
(the case where the flock roll FAILS), so non-chain outcomes still
cleanse cleanly.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, shared/combat_manager.gd,
server/server.gd. ~25 LOC.

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.598: Threat pacing — 3-concurrent cap + 10-min post cooldown
Player feedback: new dungeons spawn faster than the current ones can
be cleared, drowning posts. Two pacing knobs on the server-side
spawn path. Both gate `_create_world_dungeon` so T2+ dungeons stop
piling up on posts that already have their hands full.

1. MAX_CONCURRENT_POST_THREATS = 3. Each candidate spawn location
   checks every NPC post within 80 tiles. If any post is already at
   3 active T2+ threats, the candidate is rejected and the attempt
   loop tries again. T1 dungeons aren't gated.

2. POST_THREAT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 600 (10 min). When the last T2+
   threatening dungeon for a post is cleared (either by entering the
   tile or completing the run), the post enters a 10-min safe window.
   New T2+ spawns within 80 tiles of that post are rejected during
   the cooldown. Tracked in `_post_threat_cooldown_until` (in-memory
   only; restart-resets are acceptable for short timer).

Three new helpers: `_count_active_threats_near_post`,
`_post_in_threat_cooldown`, `_stamp_post_cooldowns_for_cleared_dungeon`.
Stamp helper called at both `completed_at` write sites.

Note: previous feedback ruled OUT player-facing real-time gates (the
v0.9.517-524 chain cooldowns). This is server-side spawn pacing
(world acts, player doesn't wait), not a player progression gate —
different shape than the ruled-out pattern.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, server/server.gd.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.597: Rank-up duplicate buttons + dungeon spillover cone
Two player-reported issues.

1. Ability rank-up popup showed duplicate option sets when a
   different ability had been ranked earlier in the session — six
   buttons total (two full sets of +1 Card / +Damage / Imprint).
   Root cause: AcceptDialog parents add_button results inside an
   internal HBox, not as direct children of the dialog, so the old
   clear-loop `for child in popup.get_children()` never saw the
   buttons. Each new popup appended NEW buttons on top of the OLD
   set. Fix: track custom buttons in a client-owned array and call
   remove_button + free synchronously before adding new ones.

2. Threat spillover from active T2+ dungeons used to be an 80-tile
   CIRCLE around the dungeon — players walking 70 tiles to the side
   still got hit with spillover encounters. Replaced with a per-
   dungeon CONE aimed at the nearest threatened post (within 80
   tiles). Cone half-angle 35°, length min(80, dist(D→P) + 15). Math
   is sqrt-free: cos²(angle) ≥ cos²(half_angle) via dot products
   and squared distances. Isolated dungeons with no post in range
   emit no cone — they're not actively threatening anyone, so they
   don't pollute the surrounding terrain.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, server/server.gd.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.596: Minigame keyboard nav + 2 bugs (loot space-skip, threat 0 reward)
Three player-reported issues batched:

1. Keyboard nav added to combat_loot_panel.gd and scratch_off_panel.gd
   so the player doesn't have to alternate to the mouse during the
   gathering/loot minigames. Arrow keys / WASD move focus across the
   reveal grid, Enter / Space activates the focused card, Tab cycles.
   Combat loot's 4x4 grid uses index-based nav; gathering's jittered
   layout uses spatial nearest-neighbor scoring (direction component
   minus a lateral penalty so 'Right' isn't fooled by diagonals). T

   had Q/Space bindings — no change there.

   victory screen because trigger_action()'s 'flock' case called

   was used for movement gating, just not for the action-bar path.

3. Threat-relief ('Drive Off the X') quests paid out 0 XP / 0 valor.
   Classic two-paths-read-same-field. The regen path in
   quest_database._regenerate_threat_relief_quest returned
   {xp: 0, valor: 0} with a comment saying real rewards came from
   extra_data.stored_rewards — but build_quest_extra_data reads
   quest.rewards to BUILD stored_rewards, so the zeros got stored
   and paid out at turn-in. Moved the tier-rewards table to
   quest_database.gd with get_threat_relief_rewards(dungeon_type)
   (tier derived from DungeonDatabase); regen path now populates
   the field. Added a self-heal in quest_manager.calculate_rewards:
   any active quest with stored_rewards={xp:0,valor:0,gems:0}
   re-derives from the current quest object at turn-in, so quests
   accepted under the broken regen path self-heal on payout.

[2026-05-22]  v0.9.595: Smoothstep blend curve for post-anchored monster levels
Fixes a level-cliff at the midpoint between two trading posts.
Player report: "lvl 16 then move one space and it jumps to lvl 25."

Root cause was the quadratic blend curve (t²) used since v0.9.567 in
world_system.gd::get_post_anchored_level. When the nearest/second-
nearest post pair swaps at the midpoint, t² evaluates to 0.25 on both
sides — but with the endpoints flipped, the result jumps by
0.5·|base_B - base_A|. Adjacent posts with ~14-30 level gaps produced
7-15 level cliffs in one tile.

Smoothstep t·t·(3-2t) evaluates to exactly 0.5 at t=0.5, so both
halves of the swap produce the same blended level — mathematically
continuous at the midpoint. Anchor pocket feel is preserved because
smoothstep is flat near both endpoints (derivative 0 at t=0 and t=1),
so each post's level still dominates its own half of the gap. The
wilderness floor and starter Lv 1-2 ring (distance 10-20) are
unchanged.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd (changelog only),
shared/world_system.gd (one-line curve swap).

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

[2026-05-21]  v0.9.592: Rank-up notification naming + log cleanup
Audit follow-up to player report: "Tactical Retreat ranked up on my
Barbarian but I don't even have that ability." Findings:

1. Tactical Retreat (internal name) displays as "Recharge" on the actual
   card. Same pattern for vanish→"Phantom Strike", pickpocket→"Steal",
   perfect_heist→"Heist". The rank-up notification used the raw internal
   id passed through capitalize-with-underscores, producing 'Tactical
   retreat ranked up!' while the card in hand showed 'Recharge' —
   players couldn't connect the two.

2. Universal abilities (Forethought, Recharge) ARE auto-added to every
   class's combat deck via initialize_deck_collection_if_needed(). This
   is design intent (the v0.9.423 hand-cycle mulligan system) but the
   #1 naming bug made it look like phantom mastery on an unowned ability.

3. Confirmed there are NO hidden / auto-triggered ability use paths.
   record_mastery_use has exactly ONE call site (combat_manager.gd:2799,
   inside process_ability_command). Passive effects, companion attacks,
   status ticks, and class racials do NOT increment mastery counters.

Fix: new _ability_display_name() helper in both client and server, used
in rank-up notification, rank_choice_applied confirmation, cull confirm,
and the rank choice popup title/body. Maps all four mismatched abilities
to their card-text names.

Also: combat_scene_panel._animate_bar_value was calling get_meta() without
a has_meta() guard, producing pre-existing log spam that the v0.9.591 HP
plumbing path made more visible. Guarded.

[2026-05-21]  v0.9.591: Complete HP discovery + estimation system overhaul
Estimates were systematically off by 50-200%% on cross-level and cross-variant
encounters; Outsmart and companion-execute kills permanently corrupted the
local cache. Root causes addressed:

1. Damage-dealt-as-HP heuristic broken by instakill paths (Outsmart, execute,
   coup-de-grace) — server now emits monster_max_hp on every victory
   (normal / outsmart / companion_clutch / heist / wish_choice / party) and
   the client stores ground truth instead of inferring from damage.
2. Cache keyed by base_name conflated different-HP variants (Shield Guardian
   1.25×, Elite 1.5×, Corrosive 1.15×) — now keyed by full variant name.
3. Estimator missed the server's hp_multiplier hyperbolic factor (2×→~6×
   across levels) and used a single-arg tiered_stat_scale assuming
   base_level=1 — rewritten to replicate scale_monster_to_level exactly:
   base_hp × tiered_stat_scale(base_lv, target_lv) × hp_multiplier(target_lv)
   × variant_multiplier. +50%% level-gap pad fudge factor removed.
4. Server-known monsters' actual_max_hp was ignored by the discovery system —
   now overrides the cache on every combat_state tick.
5. _get_base_monster_name only stripped PREFIX variants, missed SUFFIX
   ("X Weapon Master", "X Shield Guardian") and Elite both-ends ("★ X Champion")
   — all five server variant patterns now resolve correctly.

Server adds monster_base_level + monster_max_hp + monster_variant_type to
scale_monster_to_level output and forwards in combat_state, combat_end,
wish_choice, party_combat_end. Client caches base_level per base_name on
combat_start/update and reads server-truth max_hp through _discover_enemy_hp.

After v0.9.588 fixed the rank_up_choice payload dropping variant_offer,

critical bugs found and fixed.

1. NPC vendor + network buy confirmation never displayed.
   Server sent `market_buy_result` but the only client handler is
   `market_buy_success`. Purchases via the exotic / mine / farm /
   shrine vendor stocks and via network buys completed silently —

When the player out-damages a monster whose HP they didn't fully know

from "25/48" → "12/62" mid-fight. Now it stays at the known max and

[2026-05-19]  v0.9.585: ROOT CAUSE — Pathfinder turn-in npc_ prefix mismatch
After three previous attempts (v0.9.582/583/584) that all looked
correct in static analysis but didn't actually work at runtime, I
finally inspected tst3's character file on the server and spotted
the threat-bounty quest id: "threat_npc_crossroads@orc_stronghold".

The "npc_" prefix is the smoking gun.

ROOT CAUSE:

Procedural NPC posts go through world_system._normalize_npc_post
(line 3304) which prefixes their id with "npc_" — so Crossroads at
runtime is `tp.id == "npc_crossroads"` NOT bare "crossroads".

My v0.9.582/583/584 fixes all checked `tp.id in STARTER_TRADING_POSTS`
where STARTER_TRADING_POSTS contains bare names ["haven", "crossroads",
...]. So `"npc_crossroads" in [...]` was always false — the pathfinder
branch was NEVER reached. That's why my v0.9.584 diagnostic print()
never appeared in the journalctl.

FIX:

Strip the "npc_" prefix from `tp.id` before the membership check, in
all three pathfinder quest-turn-in code paths. v0.9.585 changes:
- handle_trading_post_start (line 14624): `String(tp_id).trim_prefix("npc_") in STARTER_TRADING_POSTS`
- handle_trading_post_quests (line 14847): same `trim_prefix` in the elif
- handle_quest_turn_in (line 17740): same `trim_prefix` in the if check

The diagnostic print I added in v0.9.584 was removed since the
character-file inspection cracked it.

LESSON: when a fix relies on string equality with a const list,
sanity-check the LIVE runtime value via the character file or a
print, not just the source. Two-tier post systems (legacy hardcoded
TRADING_POSTS vs procedural chunk_manager NPC posts) can have the
same post identified two different ways at different layers.

Server impact: yes — all three pathfinder checks fixed.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, server/server.gd.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

[2026-05-19]  v0.9.584: Pathfinder belt-and-suspenders + Services screen cleanup
Player reported v0.9.583's Pathfinder turn-in fix STILL wasn't surfacing
the option at the Crossroads quest board. Static analysis can't find
the bug in my v0.9.583 code path — it should work. Defensive follow-up:

1. PATHFINDER DETECTION DOUBLED UP

All three quest-turn-in code paths now accept the quest if EITHER:
- quest.chain_id == "pathfinder", OR
- quest_data.quest_id begins with "pathfinder_"

The prefix check is redundant if chain_id is populated correctly, but
catches the case where get_quest's returned dict somehow loses the
chain_id field (which I can't reproduce in static reading but the
user's report implies SOMETHING is going wrong).

Added a print() diag in handle_trading_post_quests that logs when the
branch fires so the server console will confirm whether the path is
actually being exercised on the user's next attempt.

Three sites updated:
- handle_trading_post_start summary count (line 14631)
- handle_trading_post_quests (line 14847) — adds the diag print
- handle_quest_turn_in (line 17733)

2. CROSSROADS SERVICES SCREEN CLEANUP

Pressing Back from the Quest Board used to render an outdated chat-
command-era line:
  "Services: [Q] Shop | [W] Quests | [E] Heal"
This doesn't match the actual bump-to-interact tile system. Now
cancel_trading_post_action() re-uses _display_trading_post_ui() so
the legend matches the normal post-entry view (F Forge / A Apothecary
/ Q Quest Board / etc.).

Server impact: yes — server.gd changes to two paths + one diag print.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, server/server.gd.

[2026-05-19]  v0.9.583: Hotfix — Pathfinder turn-in actually works at the Quest Board
v0.9.582 promised Pathfinder chain quests could be turned in at any
starter post. I patched 2 of the 3 quest-turn-in code paths:
- handle_quest_turn_in (line 17712) ✓
- handle_trading_post_start summary count (line 14620) ✓
- handle_trading_post_quests (line 14831) ✗ MISSED

handle_trading_post_quests is the Quest Board UI handler — the actual
path the player triggers when they bump the Q tile. Without the
parallel pathfinder branch there, the completed Pathfinder quest
never appeared in the turn-in list, leaving the player with no way
to complete the starter chain.

Direct user report: "I still have no way of turning in my starter
Quest at the questboard in the Crossroads post."

Apology baked into the changelog entry. The pattern in the codebase
(separate handlers each with their own can_turn_in logic) made it
easy to miss; v0.9.582's commit grep matched the other two but not
this one. Lesson: when fixing turn-in logic, grep for ALL
"quests_to_turn_in" callsites, not just the ones the diff caught.

Server impact: yes — third quest-turn-in code path now matches the
other two.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, server/server.gd.

[2026-05-19]  v0.9.582: Five player-reported bug fixes (Pathfinder + abandon + scroll + threat hint)
Five bugs from a single player-feedback report. Together they unblock
the starter chain, prevent accidental quest loss, fix quest log scroll
order, and ensure threatened-post info doesn't vanish.

1. PATHFINDER CHAIN TURN-IN FLEXIBILITY (closes #2 + #5)
The chain was anchored to "haven" (0,10) but new characters spawn at
(0,0) Crossroads. Players couldn't turn it in. Pathfinder quests now
accept turn-in at ANY of the 5 starter posts:
- server.gd handle_quest_turn_in: pathfinder branch added.
- server.gd quests_to_turn_in query: parallel pathfinder check so
  completed quests appear in the board's turn-in list.
- client.gd display_quest_list: hint reads "Turn in at any starter
  post" for Pathfinder, regular hint otherwise.

2. 2-STEP ABANDON CONFIRM (closes #3)
abandon_quest_by_index now requires two presses of the SAME key
within 3 seconds. First press marks pending state + warning line;
any other input cancels. Pathfinder chain quests are UNABANDONABLE
(it's the new-player tutorial). New state vars:
_pending_quest_abandon_index + _pending_quest_abandon_at_time;
cleared when leaving quest_log_mode.

3. QUEST LOG SCROLLS TO TOP (closes #4)
RichTextLabel auto-scrolls to bottom on append_text; the Chain Atlas
was getting rendered first instead of the active quests. New helper
_scroll_game_output_to_top() runs after handle_quest_log via
call_deferred so the player lands on their active quests.

4. THREATENED POST tutorial_hint MODAL (closes #1 properly)
The v0.9.580 game_output banner could scroll past. Now a strong
unmissable modal fires ONCE per character on first entry to a
threatened post. Gated by new seen_threatened_post_hint field on
Character. Explains the amber !, the +20% market markup, the +50%
service prices, and points to the THREAT BOUNTY quest.

Server impact: yes — multiple touches to handle_quest_turn_in,
quests_to_turn_in query, and trading_post_start path.

[2026-05-19]  v0.9.580: Two bug fixes from player report
Both fixes from real player feedback received mid-session:

1. PATHFINDER QUEST TURN-IN HINT NAMES DESTINATION

Active-quest list previously rendered "(Turn in elsewhere)" when the
player was at the wrong trading post. Left them guessing which post.
Now reads "(Turn in at Haven)" etc. Pathfinder's Trial is Haven-issued
but players often play around Crossroads (origin) — the new hint
points them home.

Implementation: client preloads shared/trading_post_database.gd to
read TRADING_POSTS const for name lookup. One-line change in
display_quest_list. No server change for this bug.

2. THREATENED POST ENTRY BANNER

Map shows amber "!" on Under-Threat post centers, but walking onto
the post gave no on-screen explanation of WHY the post was flagged.
Players were left to guess. (Direct user quote: "the ! at 0,0
doesn't say what it is when you go there.")

Now the trading-post entry banner reads:
  "⚠ Under Threat — <Dungeon Name> (T<n>) looms <N> tiles <direction>"
plus a follow-up line explaining the consequences (higher markups,
THREAT BOUNTY quests) and the cure (clear the dungeon). Severe
variant escalates to "⚠⚠ SEVERELY THREATENED" when 2+ T2+ dungeons
share the corridor.

Implementation: server's trading_post_start payload now includes
under_threat + threat_dungeon_name + threat_tier + threat_distance +
threat_direction + threat_severe + threat_count fields. Pulled from
the existing _compute_post_threat_state (cached per-tick + per-3s,
so zero new perf cost). Client renders the banner in
_display_trading_post_ui.

Server impact: yes — new fields in trading_post_start payload.

[2026-05-19]  v0.9.579: Patreon tame-QoL bonuses + launch doc
Follow-up to v0.9.578's Patreon scaffolding. Adds the two tame-QoL
bonuses that were scoped out of V1 + the step-by-step launch doc
for actually publishing the Patreon page.

TAME QOL BONUSES (server/persistence_manager.gd):

1. T2+ (Founder) — +1 Sanctuary registered companion slot.
   Added to get_house_companion_capacity. Stacks correctly on top
   of whatever upgrade level the player has bought. Surgical: one
   conditional added before the return.

2. T3 (Patron) — +1 free kennel-tier bump.
   Added to get_kennel_capacity. Bumps the effective level up one
   before the table lookup, clamped at the table max (500 slots).
   Saves ~5000 BP the player would otherwise spend on the first
   kennel-tier upgrade.

- Cosmetic + tame QoL only.
- NO combat advantage tied to Patreon tiers, ever.

Slot count and kennel size are convenience, not power. A Tier 3
Patron with no other upgrades has identical combat stats to a
non-supporter — they just have more storage and slots.

Step-by-step runbook for actually publishing the Patreon page:
1. Create Patreon account
2. Write About page (suggested copy included)
3. Configure three tiers
4. Set up reward fulfillment workflow
5. Add /patreon URL command to game
6. Verify in-game perks land
7. Announce
8. Maintenance ongoing

Includes the "what's already done" summary at the top so future-me
can skip straight to external setup.

Server impact: yes — capacity functions are read on every Sanctuary
load + every kennel deposit / withdraw.

[2026-05-19]  v0.9.577: Perf — DIAG_TIMING_ENABLED → false
Server-side performance pass. Flips the diagnostic timing const
that has been gating ~30+ Time.get_ticks_usec() syscalls and
conditional log_message calls per _process tick.

Context: the timing scaffolding was added during the 2026-05
freeze investigations to attribute per-region cost. Both 5s freeze
causes were fixed weeks ago (LOS cache + shared tile cache, see
project_server_freeze_investigation memo). The instrumentation is
no longer earning its keep — flipping the const to false makes the
GDScript compiler dead-code-eliminate every gated block.

Player-felt impact: small but non-zero. Frees up tiny amount of
headroom for the next perf lever (cross-render tile caching in
generate_tile's procedural noise pipeline).

Reversible: flip the const back to true and re-deploy if regressions
appear. Every diag block is gated on this single const.

Server impact: yes — touches the _process hot path.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, server/server.gd.

[2026-05-19]  v0.9.571 fix: pull_bug_reports.sh uses scp instead of rsync
Discovered while testing the bug-reporting pipeline end-to-end: rsync
isn't installed in Git Bash on Windows, so the v0.9.571 script never
actually worked on the developer's machine. Switched to `scp -r` which
ships with Windows OpenSSH.

Also tightened the empty-dir handling so the Summary section reads
honestly when there are zero reports yet (was falling through to
listing the current directory).

Pipeline verified end-to-end with a synthetic test report:
  - JSON pushed to server ✓
  - scp pulls it down ✓
  - _index.txt rebuilt with correct format ✓
The only piece untested is the client's /bug command writing the
right payload — that needs a live client interaction.

No version bump (script-only fix; client + server unchanged).

Files: scripts/pull_bug_reports.sh.

[2026-05-19]  v0.9.572: Companion Border Tier visual layer
Pairs the v0.9.570 data + stat layer with the visual rendering it was
always pitched as. Border-tier companions now have a colored ASCII
silhouette outline so the rare-border Goblin actually LOOKS rare.

of the companion's art is re-tinted to the border tier's color using
the existing MonsterArt.apply_variant_border helper. Natural body
color (variant pattern recolor) stays intact in the middle. Tier 0
(None — 60% of companions) short-circuits so no cost there.

  Common    #FFFFFF
  Uncommon  #1EFF00
  Rare      #0070DD
  Epic      #A335EE
  Legendary #FF8000
  Mythic    #FFD700

- chat-style Inspect view (client.gd)
- panel-style Inspect view (_build_companion_inspect_bbcode)
- map-hover tooltip (_build_map_companion_tooltip)

New helper _apply_companion_border_tier(art, tier) keeps the logic
DRY across sites. Tier-color array is a module-level const so each
call is a single array lookup.

Performance: zero new work for ~60% of companions (Tier 0 skip).
Border-bearing companions get one extra regex pass on the art string —
same cost as the existing variant pattern recolor.

Shadow layer (semi-transparent offset copy of the art) remains scoped
as a future cosmetic-polish slice. Border was the higher-impact half.

[2026-05-19]  v0.9.571: Bug-reporting refit — compact JSON, Linux-correct path, rsync script
Pre-existing /bug command was non-functional in production: server-side
handler wrote to C:/Users/Dexto/Desktop/Bug Reports/ — a Windows path
that silently no-op'd on the Linux Hetzner server. Zero submitted
reports actually persisted across the entire alpha test.

This release refits the data layer end-to-end:

- Client: builds a compact JSON `payload` Dict (version, UTC ts,
  player, location, character snapshot, mode flags, last ~20 game-
  output lines, pending sub-state). ≤2 KB target per report so the
  user can paste one into Claude in a single turn. Legacy text mirror
  still sent alongside for back-compat. Player-facing flow (/bug
  command + bug button) unchanged.

- Server: handle_bug_report rewritten. Writes to user://bug_reports/
  which resolves to ~/.local/share/godot/app_userdata/PhantomBadlands/
  bug_reports/ on Linux. One JSON file per report, named
  <utc-ts>_<player>.json. Augments payload with server_ts + first 8
  chars of acct_id for privacy-conscious correlation. Back-compat
  envelope wraps legacy text-only submissions.

- New scripts/pull_bug_reports.sh: rsyncs reports from production to
  local bug_reports/, rebuilds an _index.txt with one-line summaries
  (ts | player | filename | desc[:60]) so the user can scan and pick.

- New HELP_TOPICS entry: bug_report. Explains the flow, what's
  auto-captured, privacy posture, and tips for writing useful
  descriptions.

Server impact: yes — handle_bug_report rewritten + new user:// path
created on first /bug submission.

Files: VERSION.txt, client/client.gd, client/help_panel.gd,
server/server.gd, scripts/pull_bug_reports.sh (new).

[2026-05-18]  v0.9.567: Mastery cap to R6 + threat-corridor falloff sharpened
Mastery progression:
- Cap raised from R4 Master to R6 (R5 Legend +30%, R6 Mythic +45%)
- Use thresholds reduced: Novice 30→10, Adept 150→50, Expert 600→250,
  Master 2400→1200. New: Legend 4000, Mythic 10000.
- Constants updated in character.gd (source of truth) + mirrored to
  ability_panel.gd, combat_scene_panel.gd, and 5 hardcoded arrays in
  client.gd. Backfill (200 uses) now lands between R3-R4 for legacy
  characters instead of capping at R2 — small QoL bump.

Threat-corridor falloff:
- Novice ring shrunk from 20 tiles to 10 (d=10-20 = Lv 1-2)
- Easy band tightened (d=20-40 = Lv 2-6, was 30-60)
- Catch-up band wider (d=40-150 = Lv 6-50)
- Post-blend curve softened from cubic (t^3) to quadratic (t^2) so
  neighbor-post levels reach into the gap sooner

[2026-05-18]  v0.9.563: Audit #14 PvP Slice B.2 — Combat-scene PvP V1
Replaces apex auto-attack's instant dice-roll with a real turn-based
combat-scene modal where both players have agency. KO still routes to
the existing Slice D.2 sack-drop + Slice E bounty-payout flow so the
loot machinery from earlier tonight carries through unchanged.

Each round both players pick from three actions:
  - Attack:  STR×2 + weapon_dmg, ±25% variance
  - Special: max(INT,DEX)×3 + weapon_dmg/2, ±25%, mostly ignores DEF
  - Defend:  zero damage dealt; halves incoming damage this round

Simultaneous reveal — both submit, both resolve at once. Cap of 15
rounds; higher HP wins on cap. PvP HP starts at character max but is
clamped to 2000 so endgame characters don't curb-stomp newcomers.
Character's real current_hp is untouched (KO via the existing apex
respawn path).

Server (server.gd):
- active_pvp_combats Dict + _is_in_pvp_combat / _pvp_combat_id_for
- _pvp_max_hp / _pvp_basic_attack_damage / _pvp_special_damage / _pvp_apply_defense
- _start_pvp_combat (called from handle_attack_player instead of dice-roll)
- handle_pvp_combat_action + pvp_combat_action dispatch entry
- _resolve_pvp_round on both-submitted, _end_pvp_combat → _apply_apex_ko
- _cleanup_pvp_combats_for_peer hooked into handle_disconnect (forfeit)

Client (pvp_combat_panel.gd — new file):
- Modal with red border (PvP). Opponent header + HP bar at top, action
  log mid, status line + 3 buttons (Attack/Special/Defend), self HP
  bar at bottom.
- Buttons disable while waiting for opponent. Opponent-submitted hint
  shown when they're ready first. Combat closes 4s after pvp_combat_end.

Client (client.gd):
- PvPCombatPanelScript preload + instantiation alongside post_status_panel
- Action signal → pvp_combat_action server message
- pvp_combat_start/state/opponent_submitted/end message routing

Duel resolution (/duel) still uses the instant dice-roll path —
consensual duels are a separate content surface and the apex zone is
where a real combat scene matters most. Duels can be promoted to
combat-scene in a future slice if play signals it.

Help page Apex PvP line updated to describe the new modal flow.

[2026-05-18]  v0.9.562: Audit #3 Tutorial — Pathfinder's Trial starter chain
First slice of the Tutorial Initiative. New 4-stage starter chain
at Haven that walks fresh characters through a safe gather → kill
loop, rewarding a complete Tier 1 equipment kit slot-by-slot.

Chain: "Pathfinder's Trial" at Haven (chain_id `pathfinder`)
  I. Trail Rations    — fish 3 → rewards starter weapon
 II. Quarry Stones    — mine 2 → rewards starter armor
III. Mark the Edge    — kill 2 → rewards starter boots
 IV. Take the Trail   — kill 3 → rewards starter ring
                                 + Goblin egg + 100 valor bonus
                                 + Pathfinder title

Designed for zero-gear characters: tasks stay inside the starter
post bubble (Haven's fishing + ore + safe ring), each stage hands
out one Tier 1 item via the new starter_kit_slot field, and the
chain is visible to anyone who hasn't completed it (skippable for
veterans, the obvious path for newcomers).

Plumbing:
- shared/drop_tables.gd: STARTER_KIT_SLOT_MAP + get_starter_kit_item(slot)
  resolves "weapon"/"armor"/"helm"/"boots"/"shield"/"accessory" to
  Tier 1 items at level 1 (weapon_rusty / armor_leather / etc.)
- shared/quest_database.gd: pathfinder_1..4 + "pathfinder" entry in
  CHAIN_TITLES (color #9ACD32)
- server/server.gd: per-stage turn-in now resolves starter_kit_slot
  via drop_tables.get_starter_kit_item, stitches the reward line
  into the result message ahead of chain_progress_msg

Class-aware weapon split was scoped out — this game's equipment is
class-agnostic at the item level (identity comes from passives + stat
scaling), so a single weapon_rusty / armor_leather / etc. serves
every class.

[2026-05-18]  v0.9.561: Audit #12 Slice 6 — Post auto-reclaim V1
Closes the destructive cleanup loop on Audit #12. Posts that go
untended past POST_RECLAIM_THRESHOLD_DAYS (120 days) are mechanically
reclaimed by the server: walls, structures, signposts, and guards
inside the post bubble are wiped; enclosure entry removed; post slot
freed; tiles handed back to procedural generation for anyone else to
build on.

Owner gets a 14-day warning window on the visual post status panel:
"⚠⚠ AUTO-RECLAIM in Xd — visit the post to reset the timer." A single
visit anywhere in the bubble resets last_tended_at via the existing
decay-touch path. Clan-shared posts inherit the v0.9.558 wiring so
ANY clan member's visit resets the timer.

When reclaim fires, the owner (if online) gets a red chat message
plus a tutorial_hint modal explaining what happened and how to
prevent it next time.

Server (server.gd):
- POST_RECLAIM_THRESHOLD_DAYS = 120, POST_RECLAIM_WARNING_DAYS = 14
- _reclaim_player_post(owner, post_index) wipes tiles + guards +
  signpost text, _unmark_enclosure_safe + _remove_enclosure_from_tile_lookup,
  removes from player_enclosures + player_post_names + persistence,
  re-indexes remaining post enclosure lookups, refreshes bubble cache
- _check_post_reclaim_sweep() walks all posts in reverse-index order
  so removals don't corrupt iteration
- _post_reclaim_sweep_timer (5-min interval) hooked into _process()
- _compute_player_post_status_data adds days_until_reclaim +
  is_reclaim_warning + reclaim_threshold_days to the panel payload

Client (post_status_panel.gd):
- Red "AUTO-RECLAIM in Xd" banner under the inactivity line when
  is_reclaim_warning. Switches to "AUTO-RECLAIM IMMINENT" at <=0.

[2026-05-18]  v0.9.558: Audit #14 Slice F — Clan-shared posts V1
Convert-existing path. Post owner stands inside their post → `/clanpost
share` flags it as shared with their clan. All clan members then:
  - Can build structures inside the enclosure
  - Can demolish any tile inside the enclosure (regardless of who placed it)
  - Reset the post's decay timer (last_tended_at) when they walk through
    the bubble, build, demolish, or feed guards
Owner alone can `/clanpost revert` back to private.

Bubble suppression is already spatial (applies to any character in the
zone), so no extra wiring needed there. Clan share/revert notifies online
clan members so they know they can now build at the new location.

Schema:
- New clan_id field on post dicts (defaults "" = private). Persisted
  through all 3 set_player_post call sites + the rebuild-from-disk read.
- persistence_manager.set_post_clan_id helper for atomic in-place updates.

Server (server.gd):
- _post_clan_id_for / _viewer_clan_id_for_username / _can_act_on_post
- _is_in_own_or_clan_enclosure → new build gate
- _touch_clan_post_tended_at → called alongside _touch_post_tended_at at
  all 4 sites (move / build_place / demolish / feed_all)
- handle_clan_post_share / handle_clan_post_revert + dispatch entries
- _build_player_post_status surfaces the clan tag in the post header with
  the clan's banner color
- handle_build_demolish accepts clan-mates on shared posts via the
  enclosure-lookup permission check

Client (client.gd):
- /clanpost share|revert chat command (added to command_keywords)

[2026-05-18]  v0.9.556: Audit #14 PvP Slice E — Bounty system V1 (the connecting mechanic)
Per audit memo: bounties are "THE connecting mechanic — pure player-to-player
content engine." Layers on top of the apex auto-PvP loop from Slice C.

Player-funded bounties on other players. Poster pays valor upfront (locked
in escrow); bounty pays out to whoever KOs the target in the apex zone.
Multiple bounties on the same target stack — killer claims all on KO.

Commands (client.gd):
  /bounty                       — show help + your active bounties
  /bounty post <player> <valor> — pay valor upfront, lock as bounty (min 50)
  /bounty list                  — list all active bounties (sorted by total)
  /bounty on <player>           — show individual bounty postings on one target
  /bounty cancel <player>       — cancel ALL your bounties on target, full refund

Server (server.gd):
- active_bounties_on_players: target_name_lower → Array of {poster_account_id,
  poster_character_name, amount_valor, posted_at}
- handle_bounty_post: validates + deducts valor + adds entry + notifies target
- handle_bounty_list / bounty_on: read-only board renderers
- handle_bounty_cancel: refunds caller's postings on target, full amount
- _payout_bounties_on_ko: called from _apply_apex_ko after loot transfer —
  pays full pool to killer + clears bounties (closes on first KO)

Target notifications:
- bounty_posted_on_you message → red chat banner with poster + amount + total
- Bounty board sorted by total desc; online/offline tag per target

Design picks (all from user 2026-05-18):
- Posting: anyone, anytime (no anti-grief gates V1)
- Visibility: public list + private notification to target
- Pricing: poster names the amount (free-market — bigger bounties attract hunters)
- Collection: apex zone only (preserves PvE safety)
- Storage: memory-only V1 (lost on server restart, acceptable)

Server md5 c9835f586abdf2d9b00e32427770d06c.

[2026-05-18]  v0.9.553: Audit #14 PvP Slice C V1 — Apex zone auto-PvP + loot transfer
Third slice of the PvP / Bounty / Clan-shared posts big rock. Closes the
core PvP loop end-to-end in the apex frontier — players in the zone can
attack each other without consent (zone IS the consent), with valor + item
loot transferred directly on KO. Character survives; permadeath remains
PvE-only.

Mechanic:
- Action bar slot 4 shows "⚔ <PlayerName>" when adjacent to another
  player in the apex zone (priority over Fish/Mine/Chop/Quests).
- Click → server validates apex + adjacency + state, resolves instantly
  via the same dice-roll as the duel system.
- KO transfers DIRECTLY to killer's inventory:
  - 15% of victim's valor (capped at victim's actual valor)
  - 1 random equipped item (slot emptied on victim)
  - Up to 3 random non-equipped inventory items
  - Killer's inventory cap (40) respected — items lost if full
- Victim respawns at origin (0,0) with full HP, character preserved.

Slice D will add: visible loot sack tile + bump-claim mechanic + eggs
(up to 3) + 1 random non-registered companion drop. Slice B.2 will
replace the instant dice-roll with full combat-scene encounter.

[2026-05-18]  v0.9.552: Audit #14 PvP Slice B — Duel system V1 (bilateral consent + instant dice-roll)
Second slice of the PvP / Bounty / Clan-shared posts big rock. Adds /duel
chat command + mutual-consent modal + instant dice-roll resolution. Works
at ANY level / ANY zone (independent of apex zone auto-PvP coming in
Slice C).

V1 stakes:
  - "none" (default) — bragging rights only
  - "valor_10" — wager 10% of current valor (capped per player at resolution
    time so undercurrency'd players don't lose more than they have)

V1 resolution: instant dice roll. Each player rolls duel_power × randf(0.7,
1.3); duel_power = level*5 + STR*2 + DEX + weapon_damage. Higher rolled
value wins; winner gets agreed stakes from loser's valor. Both players see
the same chat line with both rolls + winner + payout.

Combat-scene PvP encounter (richer feel, turn-based card combat between
two players) deferred to Slice B.2 — V1 instant-resolve ships the loop
end-to-end so the mechanic is testable.

Usage: /duel <player>  or  /duel <player> valor

Server md5 d1d16c0dc84f7e11829beaf9bc98df7b.
