[2026-05-11]  v0.9.316: Audit #1 — Forethought + Tactical Retreat deck variants
Two new universal deck cards solve the stuck-hand problem when the 5-card
hand has only unaffordable options.

- Forethought: paid mulligan. Costs 1 of the player's primary resource.
  Discards the rest of hand, draws fresh, skips monster's turn. Setup
  card — keep the tempo.

- Tactical Retreat: free mulligan. Same discard + redraw, but the monster
  gets a free swing. Panic button when you have zero resources.

Both ship in every character's starter deck (1 copy each). Existing
characters get them backfilled on next combat via the additive pass in
initialize_deck_collection_if_needed.

details view. Locked / specialist-gated recipes fall back to the old
generic line.

Closes Layer 5 of the Audit #8 transparency roadmap.

Players can place buy orders at trading posts (Materials / Consumables /

[2026-05-11]  v0.9.309: Audit #5 Slice 12 — T7+T8+T9 boss sigs (11) — ALL COMPLETE
Final boss-signature slice. 52 signatures shipped across 12 slices.
Every dungeon boss now has a unique mechanic distinct from base
monsters and from each other.

## T7 boss signatures (4 new)
- combat_manager.gd:
  - Void Step (Void Walker): every 3 turns sets void_step_active;
    consumed in player attack path, next attack deals 0 damage.
  - Primordial Roar (Primordial Dragon): every 5 turns 20% player max
    HP + strips ALL active buffs (iterates get_active_buff_names()).
  - Coil Squeeze (World Serpent): per monster turn +1 stack (cap 10);
    each stack ticks 1% player max HP at player turn start.
  - Death Mark (Elder Lich): on first hit applies player_death_marked;
    while marked AND every 3 monster turns, deals 8% max HP.

## T8 boss signatures (3 new)
- combat_manager.gd:
  - Madness Aura (Cosmic Horror): every 4 turns sets
    player_madness_until_round = round + 2. Consumer in player turn
    start has 30% chance to fizzle the action (combat.player_can_act
    = false, return).
  - Temporal Rewind (Time Weaver): every 6 turns heals 25% max HP +
    clears monster_sabotaged and monster_weakness. Bigger heal +
    slower rhythm than Iron Discipline.
  - Reaper's Touch (Death Incarnate): 15% on-hit chance to set
    player_reaper_marked. Consumed at start of next player turn,
    dealing 15% max HP.

## T9 boss signatures (4 new)
- combat_manager.gd:
  - Chaotic Surge (Avatar of Chaos): each monster turn picks 0-5
    randomly from heal 10% / 10% max HP burst / strip random buff /
    set player_lulled / chaotic_next_dmg_mult 1.5 / chaotic_next_dmg
    _mult 0.5. Consumer in calculate_monster_damage applies the
    multiplier and clears it.
  - Unknowable (Nameless One): 25% chance in player attack path for
    damage to become 0 (no message, just nothing). Anti-pattern.
  - Divine Punishment (God Slayer): every 4 turns deals damage =
    clamp(player.level * 0.005, 0.10, 0.40) of max HP. Scales with
    player level, capped 10-40%.
  - Decay (Entropy): each monster turn +1 player_decay_stacks
    (uncapped); each stack ticks 2% max HP at player turn start.
    Fight clock — existing in combat costs HP.
- server.gd boss_ability_map: 11 new entries.
- dungeon_database.gd: renamed one ability per T7/T8/T9 boss config
  to the signature flavor name.

## Coverage — ALL BOSS SIGS COMPLETE
- 52 total signatures: 5 T1 + 8 T2 + 8 T3 + 7 T4 + 6 T5 + 7 T6 + 4 T7
  + 3 T8 + 4 T9 across 53 dungeons. (Goblin King's pre-existing Rally
  Minions covers the remaining slot.)
- Audit #5's headline OVERHAUL decision is realized.

Server deploy required.

[2026-05-11]  v0.9.308: Audit #5 Slice 11 — T6 boss sigs (7) + Phoenix Nest theme
T6 layer complete. 41 boss signatures shipped overall.

## T6 boss signatures (7 new — closes T6 layer)
- combat_manager.gd: 7 new ABILITY_BOSS_* constants.
  - Dragon's Hoard (Ancient Dragon): every 5 turns strips one player
    buff + adds a permanent +5% damage stack (uncapped). Consumer in
    calculate_monster_damage.
  - Hydra Regen (Hydra): if player damage > 10% boss max HP in single
    attack, boss heals 10% max HP. Anti-burst — sits in player attack
    path after Blood Frenzy.
  - Phoenix Rebirth (Phoenix): same shape as Death Defiance, revives at
    75% HP once per fight. Final-tier resurrection.
  - Element Cycle (Primeval Elemental): 4-phase per-round rotation:
    fire (5% burn) → water (5% resource drain) → earth (next-round
    wind shear) → air (skip turn). Reuses Wind Shear's
    player_wind_sheared_until_round and Lullaby's player_lulled flags.
  - Forge Heat (Iron Golem Overlord): on-hit +1 heat; at 5 stacks
    deals 10% max HP burst and resets. Threshold pattern — distinct
    from Hellfire Stack's compounding-per-hit. Old generic mapping
    "Forge Heat → enrage" removed from boss_ability_map (Iron Golem
    Overlord is the only carrier).
  - Riddle Curse (Ancient Sphinx): every 3 turns +1 stack (cap 5);
    each stack reduces player damage by 5%. Consumer in calculate_damage.
    Persistent stacking debuff.
  - Soul Touch (Nazgul Lord): on-hit +1 stack (uncapped); each stack
    reduces effective player defense by 2% compounding via pow(0.98, n).
    Consumer in calculate_monster_damage defense block.
- server.gd boss_ability_map: 7 new entries.
- dungeon_database.gd: renamed one ability per T6 boss config to the
  signature flavor name.

## Theme tile
- New TileType.EMBER_TILE (value 18, "o" glyph, orange #FFA500).
- _apply_theme_tags adds "phoenix_nest" branch: ~7% of EMPTY tiles
  become EMBER_TILE.
- handle_dungeon_move: stepping heals 4% max HP (capped at 80), tile
  consumed. Pairs thematically with Phoenix Rebirth.
- client.gd: tile display case 18, DUNGEON_THEME_LEGEND entry.

## Coverage
- Boss signatures: 41 total (5 T1 + 8 T2 + 8 T3 + 7 T4 + 6 T5 + 7 T6).
  T7+ untouched (11 bosses remain across T7/T8/T9).
- Theme tiles: 9 dungeons.

[2026-05-11]  v0.9.307: Audit #5 Slice 10 — T5 boss sigs (6) + Balrog Depths lava
Continues the boss-sig batch. T5 layer complete.

## T5 boss signatures (6 new — closes T5 layer)
- combat_manager.gd: 6 new ABILITY_BOSS_* constants.
  - Soul Burn (Lich): on-hit drains 5% of player's primary resource max
    via class-path match (mana/stamina/energy). Resource pressure.
  - Three Heads (Cerberus): every turn 4% max HP that ignores DEF.
    Steady defense-piercing chip.
  - Hellfire Stack (Balrog): on-hit, first burns for stacks × 4% max HP,
    then +1 stack (cap 5). Compounds across the fight — distinct from
    Festering (player-turn tick).
  - Soul Forge (Demon Lord): every 5 turns heals 15% max HP. Biggest
    periodic heal; distinct from Iron Discipline (10% + debuff clear)
    and Trollish Regrowth (8% threshold).
  - Titan Earthquake (Titan): every 4 turns 8% max HP burst + permanent
    +1 stack of -10% incoming player damage (cap 5). Escalating
    persistent defense — distinct from Stoneform's binary alt-round.
    Consumer wired in player attack path before damage subtraction.
  - Vorpal Strike (Jabberwock): every 4 turns the boss's normal attack
    deals 3x damage. Wired in calculate_monster_damage before defense
    reduction so the strike feels enormous even through gear.
- server.gd boss_ability_map: 6 new entries.
- dungeon_database.gd: renamed one ability per T5 boss config to the
  signature flavor name.

## Theme tile
- New TileType.LAVA_POOL (value 17, "^" glyph, orange #FF4500).
- _apply_theme_tags adds "balrog_depths" branch: ~10% of EMPTY tiles
  become LAVA_POOL.
- handle_dungeon_move: stepping deals 3% max HP (capped at 60).
  Persistent (lava stays). Strongest persistent-damage theme to date.
- client.gd: tile display case 17, DUNGEON_THEME_LEGEND entry.

- Theme tiles: 8 dungeons.

## T4 boss signatures (7 new — closes T4 layer)
- combat_manager.gd: 7 new ABILITY_BOSS_* constants.
  - Tremor Stomp (Giant): every 3 turns 10% max HP + sets player_lulled
    (reuses Lullaby's skip-turn flag). Burst + stun combo.
  - Blood Frenzy (Vampire): heals 30% of damage dealt back to monster HP.
    Wired in player attack path right after Phase Mirror. Distinct from
    generic life_steal (per-hit fixed %) — scales with damage dealt.
  - Hatchling Swarm (Broodmother Wyrmling): every 4 turns 15% max HP burst
    (no spawn — flavor text references already-hatched swarmlings).
  - Infernal Curse (Demon Overlord): per-turn +1 stack; at 5 stacks
    deals 25% max HP burst + reset. Bigger payload + slower fuse than
    Sonic Echo (15% / 4 stacks).
  - Talon Barrage (Gryphon Alpha): 30% on-hit chance for 2 extra attacks
    at monster.attack × 0.5 each. Chance-based and base-attack-scaled
    (avoids feedback loop with player defense).
  - Triple Threat (Elder Chimaera): cycles through three heads each
    round — poison stack / 5% burn damage / wind-shear-style slow
    (reuses player_wind_sheared_until_round flag).
  - Building Charm (Succubus Queen): on-hit +1 charm stack (cap 3); at 3
    stacks sets player_charmed (reuses existing charm self-attack flow)
    and resets to 0. Cyclical charm-burst.

  signature flavor name. Removed Wind Shear from Gryphon Aerie config
  (overlap with Harpy Matriarch signature).

[2026-05-11]  v0.9.301: Audit #6 Slice 6 — Home Stones via chain rewards + drop shift
Home Stone (Companion) was gated at monster level 251+ — the entire
early game had no way to protect a companion from permadeath. Egg/Supplies
were at 51+. By the time players could bank anything, they'd already
lost a dozen would-be favorites. This slice unblocks that.

  array of item_types. T1 chains (5 starter chains) now grant Egg +
  Companion stones on final-stage turn-in; T2 chains (6) grant Egg +
  Equipment stones. Every chain final reward becomes a complete
  permadeath-safe payout for the boss egg you just earned. Descriptions
  updated to advertise.
- server.gd handle_quest_turn_in chain-bonus block: new loop reads
  bonus.home_stones, calls drop_tables._generate_item per entry at
  character.level, adds to inventory + announces in chain_progress_msg.
- drop_tables.gd CONSUMABLE_DROPS: home_stone_egg added to T2 (level
  11-25), home_stone_egg + home_stone_supplies + home_stone_companion
  added to T3 (level 26-50). All low weight — still rare, just not
  late-game-gated. Higher-tier entries unchanged.

[2026-05-11]  v0.9.299: flock encounter log history
Players had no way to review earlier fights in a flock chain — the
combat scene panel cleared its log each time the next monster spawned,
so the [L] legacy view only showed the final fight.

- combat_scene_panel: clear_log() now takes an `archive` param; when
  true, the current log + monster header snapshot get pushed onto a
  bounded _flock_history array (cap 16). Added get_flock_history() /
  reset_flock_history() helpers.
- client.gd: _populate_combat_scene_panel passes archive=true when
  _combat_scene_was_flock_pending was true on the prior tick (i.e.
  we're chaining into the next fight). Fresh encounters reset history.
- client.gd: [L] legacy view iterates the flock history and renders
  each prior fight with its own header + "── Fight N ──" divider
  above the current fight's log.

[2026-05-10]  v0.9.273: Audit #9 Slice 3 — regional post specialization
Trading posts now have buying specialties. Travel to the matching post
for a discount on the items you're shopping for.

- mine     → -15%% on Materials (ore, gems, monster-part materials)
- farm     → -15%% on Consumables (food/herbs/fungus + potions)
- shrine   → -15%% on Runes
- fortress → -10%% on Equipment (weapons, armor)
- market   → -5%% on Equipment + Consumables + Materials (generalist)
- haven / camp / tower / exotic — no specialty (yet)

Discounts apply on TOP of the existing supply-based markup. Sellers
still receive base_valor on listing; the discount reduces only what
the buyer pays (server spread absorbs it).

Shared:
- trading_post_database.gd: POST_SPECIALTY_DISCOUNTS const, helpers
  get_specialty_discount(post_id, supply_cat) and
  get_specialty_summary(post_id).

Server:
- handle_market_buy: discount applied after markup calc on buy price.
- handle_market_browse: per-listing markup_price now reflects discount;
  per-listing `specialty_discount` field + `specialty_summary` for
  the panel header included in the response.

Client:
- New market_specialty_summary cached from browse response.
- market_panel.populate_browse takes an optional summary arg.
- New _specialty_label header rendered on browse with the summary.
- Per-row listings get a "★-N%%" badge in the price column when
  specialty_discount > 0.

Network-browse intentionally unchanged — discount applies only at the
buyer's current post since that's where the actual buy happens.

------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-05-10]  v0.9.272: Audit #1 Slice 6c — deck cull (min-1 trim)
Players can now permanently trim ability copies from their persistent
combat deck. Settings → Abilities → "− Cull" on any card with more
than 1 copy.

Resolved the open design question (min 1 vs allow 0): MIN 1. If a
player culled all copies of an ability to 0, they'd never draw that
card again → can't use the ability → can't rank up → can't earn more
copies. Soft-lock. Keeping a floor of 1 still lets players trim extra
copies they earned from rank-up "+1 Card" choices, which is the
primary deck-shaping lever.

- shared/character.gd  : cull_ability_card() with min-1 enforcement
- server/server.gd     : handle_cull_ability_card, combat-gated, saves
- client/client.gd     : signal hookup, cull_ability_card_result handler
                         (popup feedback when ability_panel is up)
- client/ability_panel.gd: per-card "Deck × N" + "− Cull" button,
                         update_deck_collection for cheap refresh

Slice 6c completes the deck-building primitives from the audit:
Slice 6a (hand/discard) + 6b (persistent deck + rank-up choice) +
6c (cull). Next: deck variants / modifiers / off-affinity counter.

[2026-05-10]  v0.9.271: rescue quest auto-route + distinct R glyph
Three changes fixing player-reported "can't find the merchant" on
Rescue the Merchant quests. Same root cause likely affected the other
RESCUE_NPC_TYPES (healer, blacksmith, scholar, breeder).

1. server.gd handle_dungeon_enter now falls back to the player's rescue
   instance (instance with entries in dungeon_npcs) when no exact
   dungeon_type match exists. Saves the quest from silently failing
   when no matching 'D' tile is near the giving post. Player gets a
   text message: "Following your rescue quest — you enter [name]. The
   [npc] is on floor N (look for R)."

2. server.gd _spawn_rescue_npc — display_char "?" → "R", display_color
   "#00FF00" → "#4DD0FF" (bright cyan). The green "?" was visually
   indistinguishable from red "?" encounter tiles, so players were
   walking past the rescue NPC entirely.

3. quest_database.gd rescue quest description updated to "Walk into
   any dungeon (D) near this trading post — you'll be routed to the
   right one. Look for the R glyph inside."

[2026-05-10]  v0.9.269: hotfix — visual market panel bulk + Food chip
Three issues player-reported on the visual Market panel after v0.9.268:

1. Bulk dropdown options silently failing.
   _on_market_panel_list_action was sending singular list_type values
   ("consumable", "tool", "material") that the server's handler
   doesn't recognize — server expects plural ("consumables", "tools",
   "materials"). Three of five Bulk options have been broken since the
   visual panel shipped. The "Invalid list type" error went to
   game_output behind the visible panel, so it looked like nothing
   happened.

   Fix: corrected list_type values AND switched to the preview-confirm
   flow introduced in v0.9.268. Player now sees the "Will list N items
   for X Valor" confirm dialog instead of silent no-ops.

2. Success / empty feedback hidden behind visual panel.
   The success path in _handle_market_list_all_success writes to
   game_output. With the visual market panel covering it, the player
   couldn't see whether anything was listed. Now: if market_panel is
   visible, show success as a popup dialog AND refresh the panel so
   new valor + listings reflect immediately. Same for the empty
   "Nothing to list" path.

3. Food filter chip missing on the visual panel.
   FILTER_CHIPS in market_panel.gd didn't include Food. Added between
   Cons and Tools — uses the same server-side filter introduced in
   v0.9.268 for the text-mode browse.

[2026-05-10]  v0.9.268: market bulk-list preview + Food filter category
Two market UX fixes player-reported on Crossroads:

1. Bulk-list preview + confirm.
   Pressing the bulk-list hotkey (1-5) on the market menu used to fire
   the listing immediately with only a brief 1.5s flash of feedback —
   easy to miss whether it actually listed anything. New flow:
   client sends market_list_preview, server computes count + total_valor
   without applying, replies with market_list_preview_result, client
   shows ConfirmationDialog "Will list N items for X Valor. Proceed?"
   On accept, client fires the real market_list_all. On empty result,
   skips the popup and logs "Nothing to list for that category."

   Server: new handle_market_list_preview mirrors the iteration logic of
   handle_market_list_all without the mutation calls. Duplicated rather
   than threading a dry_run flag through 5 branches — clearer per-path.

2. Food gets its own market filter category.
   Food materials (plant / herb / fungus / fish) were lumped under the
   "material" filter, making cooking inputs hard to find on a busy
   trading post. Browse filter now exposes "food" as its own cycle
   option. Filter does the lookup at browse time by checking the item's
   material_type against CraftingDatabaseScript.MATERIALS.type — no
   data migration needed for existing listings.

   "material" filter now excludes food (so cooking inputs don't appear
   in both). Same logic added to handle_market_network_browse so the
   cross-post network view filters consistently.

[2026-05-10]  v0.9.266: hotfix — variable-cost hotkey firing
The variable-cost rework patched the click path's affordability check
(combat_scene_panel.gd) to use the floor cost, but the action bar's
hotkey path in _get_combat_hand_actions still gated on the ceiling.
Result: a card with cost range 15-50 SP would light up castable for a
player with 35 SP (correct), and clicking would fire (correct), but
pressing the hotkey did nothing (the action's enabled flag was false).

Action bar now reads cost_floor from the ability info dict and uses
floor-if-present else ceiling for the affordability threshold —
matching the click path's logic.

Client-only fix; no server change needed.

[2026-05-10]  v0.9.265: variable-cost — Trickster utility (Audit #1, 22/23 done)
Last variable-cost batch. 4 abilities. Analyze + Vanish stay fixed-cost
(binary mechanics that don't partial-cast meaningfully).

- sabotage: ceiling 25, debuff magnitude scales; 50% stack cap unchanged.

  during the rollout).

  5-60%, so floor-cost casts are almost always a miss — intentional;
  Heist is the trickster's "commit fully" play.

[2026-05-10]  v0.9.264: variable-cost — Mage CC (Audit #1)
3 abilities. New design rule emerges: chance-based abilities scale the
chance, not the magnitude. Haste is the odd one out — speed bonus is a
magnitude, so it follows the warrior-buff pattern (magnitude scales,
duration unchanged).

- haste: ceiling 35 mana (or 3% max). Speed bonus magnitude scales by
  spend. Duration stays 5 rounds.
- paralyze: ceiling 60 mana (or 6% max). Stun CHANCE scales by spend
  (still capped at 85%, floored at 10%). Duration unchanged (1-2 turns
  if stun lands).
- banish: ceiling 80 mana (or 10% max). Banish CHANCE scales (40-75%
  raw × fraction). The 50% loot-drop chance on successful banish stays
  CONSTANT — bonus outcomes don't scale, only the headline mechanic
  (mirrors gambit's pattern: success chance vs damage swing).

18/23 abilities done. Coming next: Trickster utility (pickpocket,
sabotage, distract, perfect_heist) — the last batch.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

[2026-05-10]  v0.9.263: variable-cost — Warrior buffs (Audit #1)
5 abilities in one slice. Locked design: magnitude scales with spend,
duration stays full so buffs still "feel real" at floor cast.

- war_cry: ceiling 15, +35% damage / 4 rounds → +10% / 4 rounds at floor.
- iron_skin: ceiling 35, 60% damage reduction → 18% at floor.
- berserk: ceiling 40. BOTH the damage buff AND defense penalty scale by
  fraction ("same risk shape, smaller stakes" — like gambit). Buff range
  shrinks from +75-200% to +22-60% at floor; penalty shrinks from -40%
  to -12%.
- fortify: ceiling 25, defense magnitude scales.
- rally: ceiling 35, heal amount AND STR buff both scale.

Also fixed pre-existing client/server rally cost drift (client 45 →
aligned to server 35) in both client ability_defs and the cost-text
table.

15/23 abilities done. Coming next: Mage CC (haste, paralyze, banish) —
chance-based abilities where the design question is whether to scale
chance or magnitude.

[2026-05-10]  v0.9.260: variable-cost — Mage damage (Audit #1)
Extends Slice 6c variable-cost pattern to Blast and Meteor. Same UX
(auto-spend max-affordable, floor 30% of ceiling, partial-cast banner),
with one twist: Mage abilities use percentage-cost scaling, so floor and
ceiling both grow with the player's max mana pool.

Server (shared/combat_manager.gd):
- VARIABLE_COST_TABLE refactored to use floor_ratio (default 0.3) instead
  of explicit floor. Floor derives from ceiling × ratio after the helper
  applies racial/class/percent-scaling reductions. Same numeric outcome
  for warrior abilities (3=10×0.3, 6=20×0.3, 9=30×0.3, 15=50×0.3).
- New entries: blast (ceiling 50, cost_percent 5), meteor (ceiling 100,
  cost_percent 8). For mana entries, helper computes
  ceiling = max(base, max_mana × percent / 100) before computing floor.
- _process_mage_ability branches on VARIABLE_COST_TABLE.has() (excluding
  magic_bolt which has its own arg-driven variable path). Blast: damage
  AND burn DoT magnitude scale with fraction (duration stays 3 rounds).
  Meteor: pure damage scaling.
- Inline use_mana + Gnome/Sage cost reduction blocks removed from blast
  and meteor cases — apply_variable_cost handles all of it.

Client:
- ability_defs refactored to use cost_floor_ratio (0.3) instead of
  cost_floor. _get_ability_combat_info derives final_floor from final_cost
  × ratio after all class/race/percent modifiers — both warrior and mage
  share the same flow.
- _get_ability_cost_text shows "(f-c res)" range for all 6 variable-cost
  abilities (warrior + blast + meteor). Card UI cost label + affordability
  check use the derived floor.
- Updated blast + meteor descriptions to mention variable cost mechanic.

6/23 abilities done. Coming next: Trickster damage (ambush, gambit, exploit),
then Warrior buffs (creative "weaker for less" on duration/magnitude).

[2026-05-10]  v0.9.259: variable-cost rework — Warrior damage pilot (Audit #1)
Pilot slice extending Magic Bolt's variable-cost pattern to four Warrior
damage abilities. Each ability now has a floor + ceiling; spending the
floor yields 30% effect, ceiling yields 100%, linear between.

Server (shared/combat_manager.gd):
- New const VARIABLE_COST_TABLE: power_strike 3-10, shield_bash 6-20,
  cleave 9-30, devastate 15-50 (all stamina).
- New helper apply_variable_cost() applies skill enhancement + racial +
  class passive cost reductions to both floor and ceiling, reads current
  resource, picks spend = min(available, ceiling), computes the
  proportional fraction, spends the resource. Fails if below floor.
- _process_warrior_ability branches on VARIABLE_COST_TABLE.has() so the
  variable-cost path runs the new helper while everything else stays on
  the fixed-cost path.
- Damage scales by fraction in all 4 abilities. Shield Bash stun chance
  scales too (no full-power stun on partial cast). Cleave bleed
  magnitude scales (duration stays 4 rounds).
- Partial-cast banner ("Partial cast — N/M stamina (X% effect)") appears
  when fraction < 1.0.

Client (combat_scene_panel.gd + client.gd):
- ability_defs now carries cost_floor for variable-cost abilities.
- _get_ability_combat_info scales cost_floor with class/race passives
  (Fighter -20%, Barbarian +25%, Gnome -15%) parallel to ceiling.
- Card affordability check uses floor (cards light up if you can cast
  partial), not ceiling.
- Card cost label shows "f-c" range (e.g. "3-10 SP") for variable-cost.
- Tooltip descriptions for the 4 abilities now mention the variable-cost
  mechanic and what scales.
- Fixed pre-existing drift: client devastate cost = 60 → 50 to match
  server's ability_info.

Goal: no card is ever a brick. Subsequent slices extend to Warrior buffs,
Mage damage/utility, Trickster abilities.

  (ability_name -> copy_count) drives _initialize_combat_deck; rank-up choices
  grow it. Falls back to "1 of each accessible" if collection is empty.

[2026-05-10]  v0.9.256: combat ability deck — Slice 6a foundation (Audit #1)
Headline change: combat ability slots are now drawn from a per-fight deck
of all your accessible abilities. 5 cards in hand at a time; using a card
discards it and refills from the deck. Standard actions (Attack/Item/
Flee/Outsmart) stay always-available outside the deck. All-or-Nothing
lives permanently on R since it is too niche to draw.

shared/combat_manager.gd
- New combat_state fields: combat_deck/combat_hand/combat_discard +
  combat_hand_size = 5. _initialize_combat_deck() builds a fresh shuffled
  deck of accessible combat abilities (1 copy each); _draw_to_hand()
  refills hand and reshuffles discard when deck empties.
- process_ability_command rejects abilities not in hand and consumes the
  card from hand to discard on successful resolution.
- get_combat_display payload now carries combat_hand + deck/discard
  counts. serialize_combat_state / restore_combat preserve the deck
  across reconnect (legacy saves auto-init a fresh hand).
- COMBAT_DECK_NON_COMBAT filters teleport/cloak/all_or_nothing from the
  deck pool: cloak's L20 gate contradicts the L1-accessible rule;
  all_or_nothing is intentionally placed on R instead per user.

client/combat_scene_panel.gd
- New 5-cell hand strip between the running totals and the log section.
- Each cell shows hotkey, ability name, cost (with MP/SP/EN tag color),
  and current mastery rank. Castable cards have a gold border;
  uncastable dim. Click → card_played(name) signal.
- Hover any card → full tooltip via client_ref._get_ability_tooltip:
  cost, effect, current rank + damage modifier, progress to next rank,
  and a preview of what the next rank gives.

client/client.gd
- combat_hand state mirrors server pushes; refreshed on combat_start
  and every combat_update. _process_combat_start now calls
  update_action_bar() AGAIN after the hand is parsed so keys 1-5 bind
  to the freshly drawn cards on turn 1 (was leaking the legacy
  equipped_abilities mapping).
- _get_combat_hand_actions() builds [A/N, hand0..4] for the action
  bar's ability slots — A/N permanently on R, cards on keys 1-5.
  card_played signal routes to send_combat_command (with variable-cost
  prompt for Magic Bolt / etc).
- Inventory: double-click an item → equip (if equipment) or use (if
  consumable). Materials/locked items fall through silently.
- Description audit: ~19 ability descriptions corrected against the
  combat_manager impl (Forcefield was "blocks 2 attacks" → actually
  flat-damage shield 100 + INT * 8). Trickster strings still queued
  for verification in the next pass.
- Tooltip rank-up preview: damage abilities show "Next rank N: damage
  modifier +X%"; utility/buff/CC abilities get an honest "no direct
  damage bonus (utility ability)" line.

server/server.gd
- handle_create_character now sets ability_uses_backfilled = true
  unconditionally so freshly made characters skip the existing-character
  rank-2 migration. New chars start at Rank 0 (Untrained, -20% damage),
  matching Slice 1's "weak at first, grow with use" direction.

shared/world_system.gd
- NPC post map header: compass is now appended inline with the "Safe"
  marker (matches the wilderness path) so the header is 2 lines, not 3.
  The 3-line layout was making the player class sprite render one tile
  above its actual @ position because the client overlay assumes 2.

egg per monster lineage.

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.250: quest chains foundation — Goblin Menace (Audit #6 Slice 1)
First quest chain shipped using new chain-quest schema. Each chain
quest in QUESTS dict carries chain_id / chain_stage / chain_total /
next_in_chain / chain_bonus. One chain defined: "The Goblin Menace"
at Haven, 3 stages (Kill 5 Goblins → Kill 3 Hobgoblins → Slay Goblin
King). Final reward: 240 bonus valor + Goblin Egg + base XP/valor.

Server flow: get_chain_starters_for_post() filters available chain
starters at trading posts (skipping completed/in-progress chains).
On quest turn-in, mid-stage auto-adds the next quest to active with
a chat announcement; final-stage applies chain_bonus and pushes
chain_id into character.completed_chains. New @export var
completed_chains on Character with serialization hooks.

No new client UI needed — chain quests render through the existing
quest UI; chain badge inline in description via BBCode. UI polish
and more chains in future slices. This is the foundation for the
audit's broader quest overhaul (multi-path completability, title
rewards, replacing daily refresh).

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.249: boss signatures Slice 4 — Iron Discipline (Hobgoblin Commander)
Hobgoblin Commander (Hobgoblin Fortress T2) gets Iron Discipline:
every 5 monster turns, boss heals 10% max HP and clears its own
sabotage/weakness debuffs. Distinct from generic regeneration
(per-turn flat) — periodic burst that punishes long fights and
re-rewards short-kill builds.

Tracks via combat.iron_discipline_last_round to prevent double-fire
within the same turn (multi_strike etc.). New ABILITY_BOSS_IRON_DISCIPLINE
constant, "Iron Discipline" flavor mapping, Hobgoblin boss config
opts in. 7 boss signatures shipped total.

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.248: Spider Nest webbed tiles — theme tags Slice 1
First cut of the audit's per-dungeon theme tags system. New
TileType.WEBBED (display 'w', purple). _apply_theme_tags
post-processor runs at the end of generate_floor_grid; for
spider_nest, ~10% of empty tiles become webbed.

Stepping onto a webbed tile costs +1 extra dungeon step (server
move handler). Player gets a chat note. Webs persist all fight,
re-crossing pays again — Spider Nest now has a real mechanical
texture distinct from other T2 dungeons.

Pattern is in place: future themes (Forgotten Crypt revives,
Mimic Treasury chests-that-bite, Plague Graveyard poison aura,
Harpy Cliffs falling tiles) plug into _apply_theme_tags without
touching the BSP generator.

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.247: boss signatures Slice 3 — Bloodscent + Festering Bite
Two more boss mechanics, two more bosses (covers all 5 T1 dungeons):

- Alpha Wolf (Wolf Den T1): Bloodscent — when player drops below
  50% HP, boss gains +50% damage rest of fight. Mirrors Orc Warlord's
  Bloodied Fury but inverted to trigger on player HP, pack-flavor.

- Rat King (Rat Warrens T1): Festering Bite — each monster hit adds
  +1 festering stack (max 5). Each stack ticks 2% max HP per player
  turn. Distinct from generic Bleed (fixed value, single application).
  Snowballs if you let the rat king hit you repeatedly.

New ABILITY_BOSS_BLOODSCENT and ABILITY_BOSS_FESTERING_BITE
constants. Festering tick added to player turn alongside bleed
tick. Total boss signatures shipped: 6.

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.246: trading post rumors — discoverability Slice 2
Each trading post now overhears up to 2 nearby dungeons within
150 tiles and surfaces them as gossip when the player enters:
"They say Spider Nest (T2) lies 38 tiles southeast." Free public
knowledge with cached generation per post, 30-min refresh.

Cached in trading_post_rumors dict (server-side) so all visitors to
the same post see the same rumor set within the cache window. Skips
other players' personal-instance dungeons.

Pairs with v0.9.244's Dungeon Compass: rumors are public/free at
trading posts, compass is a consumable at the player's location.
Two complementary discoverability tools.

Two more boss signatures, two more bosses:

- Orc Warlord (Orc Stronghold T2): Bloodied Fury — one-shot trigger
  when boss drops below 30% HP, +75% damage rest of fight. Distinct
  from Berserker (50% threshold, +50%, every-turn) — sharper threshold,
  sharper peak.

- Grand Mimic (Mimic Treasury T2): Treasure Decoy — guaranteed 2x
  first-attack crit. Distinct from Ambusher (75% chance, 1.75x).
  Set up via treasure_decoy_pending in start_combat. Telegraphs as
  the trap it is, not a stealth strike.

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.244: dungeon compass consumable — discoverability Slice 1
New consumable item targeting the audit's #1 player pain ("finding
dungeons is the most difficult time"). Three tiers gated by tier_max:
standard <= T2, Greater <= T5, Master <= T9. Drops at 18% chance
from treasure chests tier 2+, with compass tier_max scaling to
chest tier so the range matches the player's progression.

On use, server _use_dungeon_compass picks the nearest world dungeon
within the compass's tier scope and chats name/tier/direction.
Sends a compass_reveal payload (x/y/name/color) the client can
later render as a temporary map marker. Compass not consumed
when no qualifying dungeon is in range — avoids wasted-feel.

Combat-deny list updated so compass doesn't appear under "Use Item"
during fights. Inspect description added.

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.243: boss signatures Slice 1 — Death Defiance + Constricting Web
Audit #5 Slice 1: each boss gets one mechanic distinct from its base
monster pool. First two bosses wired:

- Skeleton Lord (Forgotten Crypt T1): Death Defiance — revives once
  per fight at 50% HP when killed. Hooked early in
  _process_victory_with_abilities so it intercepts the death cascade
  and continues combat.

- Spider Queen (Spider Nest T2): Constricting Web — replaces flavor
  Web Trap (slow_aura). 25% chance on hit to web the player; WIS/4
  reduces chance, floor 10%. Webbed players skip their next turn.
  Re-rollable on future hits.

New ABILITY_BOSS_REVIVE_ONCE and ABILITY_BOSS_WEB_STUN constants.
Flavor names "Death Defiance" / "Constricting Web" mapped via
boss_ability_map in server.gd. Boss configs in dungeon_database.gd

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.242: mastery Slice 3 — Sanctuary headstart purchases
New Sanctuary panel (house_mode == "mastery") lets players spend
baddie points to start their next character with rank already in
specific abilities. Steep cost curve: 25/100/500 BP per rank step,
cumulative 625 BP for a fully maxed-headstart on one ability. Hard
cap at R3 — R4 (Master) always earned through play.

Persistence adds pending_headstarts to each account and a
consume-on-create flow. Single-key cycle UX: 1-5 bumps the row's
rank, wrapping to refund-all when at cap. No-record gate prevents
buying ranks for abilities the account has never reached.

apply_headstart_ranks on Character sets ability_uses[ability] to
the threshold for the queued rank, sets ability_uses_backfilled
so the legacy migration doesn't double-stamp.

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.241: mastery Slice 2 — account-level rank persistence
New mastery_records dict per account. Highest rank ever for each
ability persists at the account level across permadeath. Combat
rank-ups update the record live via a new mastery_rank_changed hook
on the combat result. _award_baddie_points_on_death snapshots all
non-zero ranks as a safety net for backfilled archetype ranks that
never trigger an in-combat rank-up signal.

Death screen now lists "Mastery Records Preserved" with each
ability + rank. character_list payload carries the records so the
client caches them in account_mastery_records, ready for Slice 3
(Sanctuary headstart purchases).

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.237: mastery polish — slower grind, tooltips, anti-spam cap
Slice 1 polish round responding to the first playtest. Thresholds 3x'd,
per-combat use cap added, hover tooltips on ability cards.

Thresholds:
- MASTERY_RANK_THRESHOLDS bumped from [10, 50, 200, 1000] to
  [30, 150, 600, 2400] in shared/character.gd, shared/constants.gd, and
  client/ability_panel.gd. Existing characters' rank-2 backfill of 200
  uses still lands at rank 2 (baseline) under the new curve so no
  migration needed.

Per-combat use cap:
- New MASTERY_USES_PER_COMBAT_CAP = 5 in combat_manager.gd. The mastery
  hook now tracks combat["mastery_uses_this_fight"][ability] and only
  calls character.record_mastery_use when the count is below the cap.
  Past the cap the ability still works normally — it just doesn't
  credit rank progress. Stops grind-spam (e.g., Magic Bolt at 5 mana
  repeated 30 times); the eventual deck-building model will make this
  cap redundant when draw-N-per-round naturally bounds usage.

Hover tooltips:
- New _get_ability_description_text + _get_ability_tooltip in client.gd
  compose plain-text tooltips with name / cost / effect / current rank /
  damage modifier / progress to next rank. Mirrors the desc strings in
  shared/constants.gd MAGE/WARRIOR/TRICKSTER_ABILITIES.
- ability_panel.gd's _tooltip_for callback delegates to the client.
  Slot cards (top row) and grid cards (full ability list) both set
  card.tooltip_text so a hover surfaces the info without clicking.

Side fix:
- "More → Abilities" wording in the changelog and a stale comment
  corrected to "Settings → Abilities" (saved to memory so it doesn't
  recur).

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.236: combat ability mastery Slice 1 — use-progression foundation
Audit #1 kicked off. Replaces the fixed level-unlock model with a
use-based mastery progression. All 24 abilities are accessible from L1;
effective power scales with how much each ability has been used.

shared/character.gd:
- New ability_uses: Dictionary + ability_uses_backfilled flag, both
  serialized.
- MASTERY_RANK_THRESHOLDS [10,50,200,1000] + MASTERY_RANK_DAMAGE_MULT
  [0.80, 0.90, 1.00, 1.10, 1.20] + MASTERY_RANK_NAMES
  ["Untrained","Novice","Adept","Expert","Master"]. Rank 2 = baseline so
  existing characters can be backfilled to that point with no balance
  shock.
- get_ability_rank / get_ability_damage_mult / get_ability_uses_to_next_rank
  / record_mastery_use / backfill_ability_uses_if_needed helpers.
- get_unlocked_abilities() now returns all abilities (level gate removed).

shared/constants.gd:
- Mirror constants for visibility / non-character callers.

shared/combat_manager.gd:
- Removed the three "Check level requirement" branches in _process_mage/
  _warrior/_trickster_ability — rank scales effective power instead.
- apply_skill_damage_bonus now multiplies mastery mult on top of the
  legacy skill_enhancements bonus. Magic Bolt + Power Strike use this
  hook today; remaining 9 damage abilities track usage and rank up but
  don't apply damage mult yet (Slice 1b extends them).
- process_ability_command success path calls character.record_mastery_use
  and appends a colored rank-up announcement to result.messages.

server/server.gd:
- handle_select_character calls character.backfill_ability_uses_if_needed
  on first load so existing characters don't suddenly take a -20% nerf.
  One-shot guard prevents re-stamping on subsequent logins.

client/client.gd + client/ability_panel.gd:
- ability_panel.populate now takes ability_uses; slot cards and grid
  cards render "RN Name (uses/threshold)" or "RN Name *" at cap.
- Ability-cost meta line is replaced with cost + rank/progress.
- Rank-up announcement renders inline in the combat log (piggybacks on
  result.messages, no new message handler required).

Slice 1 limitations (deferred):
- Damage mult only on Magic Bolt + Power Strike (Slice 1b hooks remaining
  damage abilities).
- No account-level persistence yet (Slice 2).
- No Sanctuary headstart purchases (Slice 3).
- No off-affinity counter (Slice 4).
- No deck pruning / ability modifiers (Slices 5-6).

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.235: building-template kits — Small Enclosure Kit
Closes the build-flow loop opened by post-anchored world Slices 4-5: a
single craftable kit drops a full 5x5 enclosure with a south-facing door
in one press, replacing the per-tile build grind for the common case.

server.gd:
- New KIT_LAYOUTS const dict; enclosure_kit_small layout has 16 walls
  + 1 south door, all offsets relative to the player's tile.
- _craft_structure flags any output whose structure_type is in
  KIT_LAYOUTS as is_kit: true and stamps the recipe description.
- handle_build_place branches to _handle_kit_place when item.is_kit;
  direction is ignored (the kit always anchors at the player's tile).
- _handle_kit_place pre-validates world bounds, NPC-post proximity
  (3-tile buffer matching the single-tile build path), and dungeon
  entrance proximity for the entire layout before placing any tile, so
  a forbidden-zone failure never half-stamps walls. Per-tile water/void
  /other-player-ownership checks via _gm_place_owned_tile then place
  what's possible, skipping conflicts. Consumes one kit and triggers
  _check_enclosures_after_build so the placed walls form a proper
  player post immediately.

shared/crafting_database.gd:
- New craft_enclosure_kit_small recipe (Construction skill 8, Builder
  specialist, 32 stone block + 4 wooden plank + 2 iron ore — slight
  convenience tax over crafting the loose tiles individually).

client.gd:
- New build_active_is_kit state mirrors build_active_structure_type;
  set in _select_build_item from item.is_kit.
- display_build_direction shows kit-specific copy ("Drops the kit's
  layout centered on your tile. Press any direction to place.")
  instead of the per-tile WASD prompt.
- handle_build_result kit branch: on success, exit build mode (player
  is inside the new enclosure and can't place another there); on
  failure, keep the prompt visible so the player can move and retry.
- Cleared in all build-exit paths alongside build_active_structure_type.

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.234: Slice 5 — spawn-at-post in character creation
New characters can pick a player-owned post on their account as their
spawn point, instead of always starting at Crossroads. The user-facing
payoff of the post-anchored world initiative — your old character's
hard-built post becomes a level-friendly home for your next character.

server.gd:
- account_id stamped on player posts going forward, threaded through all
  4 mutation paths and gm_build_test_post.
- _rebuild_enclosures_for_player backfills account_id at server start
  by looking up the username's current account_slot via the new
  persistence.find_account_for_character helper.
- _get_posts_for_account(account_id) returns owned posts as
  {owner, post_index, name, x, y, tier, effective_tier, bubble_radius}.
  Attached to the existing character_list message as
  available_spawn_posts.
- handle_create_character reads optional spawn_post_owner +
  spawn_post_index, validates the post.account_id matches the
  requesting account, and sets character.x/y to the post center.

client.gd:
- Caches available_spawn_posts from character_list.
- show_character_create_panel injects a SpawnOption OptionButton +
  SpawnDescription RichTextLabel programmatically into the .tscn-defined
  panel (first entry is Origin/Crossroads default; others are the
  account's posts with tier/coords/owner/bubble metadata).
- Spawn choice is sent in create_character only when not Origin.

Limitation: posts whose creator died and was cleared from
character_slots before this update have no account_id and stay orphaned
(can't be selected as spawn). Posts created by currently-alive
characters get backfilled automatically at server start.

Placing a structure no longer kicks the player back to the inventory.

out or the player presses Cancel. Same loop applies to demolish.

  _find_inventory_idx_by_structure_type() re-resolves the inventory slot
  at every send_to_server, so a character_update arriving between the
  last placement and the next keypress can't leave build_selected_item

  state var so it doesn't leak.

Adds three admin commands and a new World page in /admin so Slice 4 can be

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.229: post-anchored world Slice 4 — active settler bubbles
Player post settler bubbles now actually suppress monster levels, gated by
guard count (with tower bonus) and food maintenance.

world_system.gd:
- TIER_REFERENCE_DISTANCE + level_for_tier(tier) maps a post tier to the
  monster level it anchors at, using the radial _distance_to_level curve
  evaluated at the typical post distance for that tier.
- player_post_bubbles cache + update_player_post_bubbles() setter.
- get_post_anchored_level consults bubbles first; inside one, uses
  level_for_tier(effective_tier) instead of the trading-post lerp.

server.gd:
- _compute_effective_post_tier(post_meta) — moderate formula:
  effective = clamp(wilderness - guards - tower_guards, post.tier, wilderness).
  Each guard owned by post owner inside bubble = -1; tower-adjacent
  (existing in_tower flag) = additional -1.
- _update_player_post_bubble_cache() pushes the per-post bubble array to
  world_system. Piggybacked onto _update_guard_cache so hire/dismiss/decay
  refresh automatically; explicit calls added for enclosure mutations.
- Region indicator override now uses effective_tier instead of floor tier.

Tutorial dependency: added step 13 to project_tutorial_initiative.md so
new-player onboarding teaches enclosures + guards + bubble suppression.

[2026-05-09]  v0.9.227: post-anchored world Slice 2 — monster-level model rewire
Monster level generation now flows through get_post_anchored_level(x, y),
which lerps between the two nearest trading posts' radial anchors and uses
the radial _distance_to_level curve as a wilderness floor. At every post
center the anchor equals the radial value at that post's origin distance,
so balance is unchanged in the current world layout. Architectural rewire
that unlocks Slice 3+ (player posts overriding anchors) and Slice 6+
(per-tier base-level tuning). Smoke test in tools/test_post_anchored.gd.

[2026-05-08]  v0.9.224: dungeon chests slice 2 — final chest + boss-teleport defer
Boss kill no longer auto-teleports. Instead:
1. _complete_dungeon's first call now early-returns after placing a
   FINAL_CHEST tile adjacent to the player (the boss died and the
   player is on or near the boss spot). Server tracks this in a
   peer-keyed pending_final_chest dict + sends an updated
   dungeon_state with awaiting_final_chest=true.
2. Walking onto the * tile triggers _open_final_chest — guaranteed
   tier-appropriate equipment (rolled twice, better-rarity kept),
   1-3 sub-tier-scaled materials, 1 dungeon-exclusive consumable,
   and 3-5x typical-fight Valor. Then re-calls _complete_dungeon
   which now bypasses the gate (peer is in pending_final_chest)
   and runs the existing teleport + dungeon_complete flow.
3. New "Leave Now" action bar button when awaiting — fires
   dungeon_skip_final_chest message which calls _complete_dungeon
   directly without bonus loot.

New TileType.FINAL_CHEST (orange * glyph) added to dungeon_database
enum + tile char/color tables, plus the matching client renderer.

Reconnect edge: if the peer disconnects mid-pending and reconnects,
walking onto the chest tile re-adds the in-memory entry rather than
no-op'ing. Persistent reconnect storage is a follow-up.

[2026-05-08]  v0.9.223: dungeon overlay fix + admin instant-dungeon entry
Dungeon view was rendering a stray world-map sprite + companion off
to the side of the dungeon grid. The class-sprite overlay layer
gates on `game_state == PLAYING and not in_combat` but not on
`dungeon_mode` — same class of bug as the v0.9.218 / v0.9.219
char-select / Sanctuary fixes. Add `not dungeon_mode` to the gate
and call `_sync_map_sprites_overlay()` on dungeon enter / complete /
exit so the overlay hides going in and reappears coming out.

Plus: new admin shortcut `gm_enter_dungeon` (server) +
"Enter T1/T6/Tier-Appropriate Dungeon" buttons (client admin
panel). Server picks an appropriate dungeon by tier param (or by
player level when tier=0) and calls handle_dungeon_enter with
confirmed=true, dropping the player into a fresh personal instance.
No walking to a 'D' tile, no spawn waits — fixes the user feedback
that finding a suitable dungeon for testing the v0.9.222 chest
slice was painful.

[2026-05-07]  v0.9.222: Dungeon chests slice 1 — equipment + dungeon-exclusive consumables
Treasure chests now roll a tier-appropriate equipment piece (55%
chance) on top of the existing materials/egg/scroll/recipe rewards.
This is the "equipment slightly more common in dungeons" knob —
chests are the answer, monster drop tables stay untouched so
overworld fights aren't affected.

Three new dungeon-exclusive consumables (chest-only, 25% per chest,
gated by tier):

- Boss-Slayer Tonic — +30% damage vs boss-tagged monsters, 1 battle.
  Plugs into the existing persistent_buff system; calculate_damage
  checks character.get_buff_value("boss_damage") against
  monster.is_boss.
- Reclaimer's Lantern — +25% chance for an extra item drop on next
  5 dungeon kills. Combat-victory loot path checks the buff and
  rolls a bonus drop on dungeon combat only.
- Floor Skip Charm — out-of-combat in a dungeon, advances to the
  next floor instantly. Errors on boss floor / outside dungeon.

Tier-1 chests only drop the Charm; Lantern unlocks at T2, Tonic
at T4. Helpers live in drop_tables.roll_dungeon_chest_equipment /
roll_dungeon_chest_consumable, called from server's chest-open
handler so existing roll_treasure stays focused on its old job.

Final chest + boss-teleport-defer + trap chests come in slice 2 —
this slice ships the equipment density knob and the new items
without the dungeon-flow surgery.

Easier testing: /admin → Items → "Test Dungeon Chest Drops" gives
one of each new consumable plus a T6 equipment piece.

[2026-05-07]  v0.9.219: Sanctuary stale-sprite + bigger ASCII
Two fixes:

1. Sanctuary screen (HOUSE_SCREEN) was leaving the previous character's
   sprite + companion visible to the right of the home view. v0.9.218
   fixed the same bug for the char-select panel; this extends the fix
   to Sanctuary entry by calling _sync_map_sprites_overlay() from
   show_house_panel(). Game state is HOUSE_SCREEN, not PLAYING, so the
   sync's "not should_show" branch hides the leftover sprites.

2. Player class ASCII art was too small everywhere it rendered. Bumped
   ClassAsciiArt.DEFAULT_FONT_SIZE from 3 to 4. This propagates to:
   - Battle scene player slot: 3 → 4
   - Inspect / status page: 3 → 4
   - Player-list popup: 2 → 3 (uses get_font_size - 1)
   Map hover tooltip was hard-coded at 2; bumped directly to 3.

[2026-05-07]  v0.9.218: appearance variants follow-up fixes
Three bugs reported after v0.9.217 ship:

1. Map hover tooltip ASCII didn't match the variant the player sees in
   the battle scene. Cause: appearance fields weren't plumbed through
   `get_nearby_players` (server) or the local map sprite metadata
   (client). Tooltip code's appearance lookup found nothing and fell
   back to plain rendering. Fixed both paths — server entries now
   include appearance_variant/color/color2/pattern, and the local +
   remote sprite metadata reads them through.

2. Player-list popup ASCII art was skewed horizontally because
   player_info_content uses a proportional font by default — variable-
   width chars made the columns drift. Wrapping the art in a
   `[font=res://font/Consolas/consolas.ttf]` BBCode tag forces the
   same monospace render the battle scene uses.

3. Returning to character select left the previous character's sprite
   + companion visible to the right of the Sanctuary preview. The
   _sync_map_sprites_overlay() function only ran on world-map location
   updates, so without one firing on the transition the sprites stayed.
   Now show_character_select_panel() calls it explicitly, hitting the
   `not should_show` branch (game_state != PLAYING) which hides
   everything.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

[2026-05-07]  v0.9.217: player appearance variants — random palette/pattern per character
Each new character rolls a random color + pattern from the EGG_VARIANTS
pool companions use. Variant sticks to that character for life. New
characters on the same account roll fresh.

Variant drives the recolor of the player's class ASCII art at every
surface that shows it:
- combat scene panel (player slot)
- map hover tooltip
- inspect / status page
- player-list popup (clicking another player)

Solid patterns recolor as a single hue; gradient/striped/middle/edges/
diagonal/etc. patterns do per-line/per-char recoloring through the same
_recolor_ascii_art_pattern helper companions use.

Existing characters get a one-time backfill in Character.from_dict on
next login — no manual migration needed. After that the variant is in
to_dict and persists normally.

New character fields (additive):
  appearance_variant   String  e.g. "Crimson", "Volcanic"
  appearance_color     String  primary hex
  appearance_color2    String  secondary hex (gradient/middle); empty for solid
  appearance_pattern   String  solid | gradient_down | gradient_up | etc.

Server: rolls on handle_create_character, ships fields in player_list
(both handle_get_players and broadcast_player_list) + handle_examine_player
results so other players see the same variant.

Client: combat_scene_panel.populate gets new player_appearance_* keys and
plumbs them into _refresh_player → set_player_ascii_art. Inspect, map
hover (_build_map_player_tooltip), and player-list popup
(show_player_info_popup) read appearance fields and apply the recolor.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-05-07]  v0.9.216: flock-encounter freeze hotfix
show_flock_warning() created an infinite-loop alpha pulse tween bound
to a label, but hide_flock_warning() only freed the label — leaving
the tween orphaned. On the next monster turn the tween would step
against a freed node, hit the 0-duration infinite-loop guard at
scene/animation/tween.cpp:406, and lock the frame.

Now the pulse tween is stored as _flock_warning_pulse_tween and
explicitly killed in hide_flock_warning() before the label is freed.

Bug was pre-existing (since the flock-warning banner shipped) but
became visible during long flock chains. User-visible symptom: client
freezes on the "More wolves approaching! Press Space to continue"
screen, ERROR: Infinite loop detected spam in console.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

[2026-05-06]  v0.9.211: Item quality cleanup
Dropped potions, scrolls, elixirs and bane potions are now always Common
rarity. Previously rarity was rolled (Uncommon..Legendary..Artifact) but
combat use ignored the rarity-based potency multiplier — only out-of-combat
use applied it. The mismatch made 'Rare Health Potion' heal the same as
'Common Health Potion' in combat, which read as broken to players. Tier
remains the visible progression for dropped consumables.

Tomes, Home Stones, and Mysterious Boxes keep their rarity since those are
meaningful tiers for those item categories.

Crafted potion bug fix: out-of-combat use now actually applies the quality-
scaled amount shown on hover. A Masterwork Health Potion with effect.amount
= 187 now restores 187 HP when drunk from inventory. Previously the
inventory-use path read effect.base from POTION_EFFECTS (always 0 for tier-
based types) and ignored the item's own quality-scaled effect dict.

Same fix applied to crafted resource potions (mana / stamina / energy).
Crafted buff scrolls noted as a follow-up — their inspect description
shows recipe amount but combat applies a stat_pct formula; bigger rework.

[2026-05-06]  v0.9.210: Combat Juice Phase B1 — companions can be wounded and KO'd
Companions get a real combat HP pool (30 + level*5 + sub_tier*10 + hp_bonus)
shown as a red bar under the companion ASCII. 25% chance per monster turn to
swing at the companion instead of the player; floating damage label fires
over the companion ASCII on those hits.

KO at 0 HP: companion grays out, stops attacking, monster stops targeting.
KO state PERSISTS between fights. KO'd companions can ONLY be revived by a
healer NPC or healer station — not potions, rest/meditate, or movement.

Healing potions can target the companion in combat via a Self/Companion
in-panel picker. Healer NPC + station heal the companion alongside the
player at the same valor cost. Companion HP regenerates passively at the
same rate as the player on overworld move (1%/step), dungeon move
(0.5%/step), Rest (10-25%), and Meditate (10-25%) — skipped while KO'd.

Healer encounter UI now shows the companion's HP and a '(revives + heals
<name>)' tag on each option. Rest/Meditate messages call out companion
recovery or KO state explicitly.

Sanctuary sub-modes:

- Storage tab: capacity bar, item list, right-click any row for
  Mark for Withdraw / Register as Companion / Discard. Discard goes
  through a confirmation dialog. Confirm Withdraw / Clear Withdraw
  buttons commit or reset the queue.
- Upgrades tab: Base / Combat / Stats sub-tabs, each upgrade as a
  card showing current/max level, description, current effect, and a
  Buy button labelled with the BP cost (disabled when maxed or
  unaffordable).

Visibility syncs with HOUSE_SCREEN + house_mode in {storage, upgrades}.
The walkable map view, Kennel (K), and Fusion (F) remain on the existing
text path for now.

[2026-05-05]  v0.9.195: Visual companions + eggs panel (UI Facelift Phase 4)
Adds CompanionsPanel — tabbed surface for the More → Companions and
More → Eggs screens. Companions tab has the active companion at the
top (XP bar + Dismiss button) and a grid of hatched companion cards
below; left-click activates, right-click opens an Inspect / Release /
Activate menu. Inspect view replaces the body with full ability and
bonus details and exposes a Release button with Back. Eggs tab shows
each incubating egg as a card with its ASCII art, tier, progress bar,
and frozen status — click to toggle freeze.

Sort cycler (Level / Tier / Variant / Damage / Name / Type) with an
asc/desc toggle in the top-right.

Sanctuary (HOUSE_SCREEN) is intentionally out of scope for this slice —
it's a separate game state with multiple sub-modes (storage, kennel,
fusion, upgrades) and warrants its own phase.

card grid panel built from new ItemCard, EquipSlot, and InventoryPanel
nodes.

  stat breakdown and live deltas vs. equipped gear

  saved username exists, password field is focused on the login screen

[2026-04-22]  v0.9.189: Station placement is now truly random within the post interior
Previously _place_stations shuffled the station type ORDER but still
placed them on a fixed row-by-row grid starting at the top-left. The
visible layout barely changed between posts.

Now every station is placed on a random interior floor tile, seeded
deterministically by post coords. Constraints:
  • No two stations cardinally adjacent (1-tile walkable gap)
  • Tiles adjacent to any door are excluded
  • Post marker at center is reserved
  • Crossroads throne still anchored at south-center

Fallback loop drops the adjacency rule if the post is too small for all
19 stations + spacing, so cramped posts still get everything placed.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

[2026-04-21]  v0.9.188: Market stacks, post corners, station variety, respawns, keybind conflicts
- Market listings: listing a stack (5x Minor Health Potion) now correctly
  records quantity=5 and base_valor*5 instead of dropping the rest. Applies
  to single list + "list all consumables/tools" bulk actions.
- Post corners: _compute_wall_tiles now uses 8-way adjacency so outer
  corners are walls — players can no longer slip in diagonally past the
  doors.
- Station layout: legacy posts now delegate to _place_stations (the
  shuffled layout) instead of hardcoding Forge/Blacksmith/Healer/Market
  positions. All posts now have varied station arrangements.
- Resource respawn: POST_RESOURCE_RADIUS 12→22, POST_NODE_RESPAWN_TIME
  900s→240s. Resources near posts now regrow every ~4 minutes within a
  wider radius.
- Keybind conflicts: rebinding a key that's already used now shows a Y/N
  warning and auto-moves the displaced action to an unused key on confirm.
  Fixes the "reassigning Up to W makes Down unreachable" trap.

[2026-04-21]  v0.9.187: Egg HUD %, freeze click, dungeon complete timing, meditate clarity
- Egg % stuck at 0 — display was reading a non-existent "steps" field;
  now derives progress from hatch_steps - steps_remaining (HUD + two
  death/leaderboard snapshot views)
- Egg freeze click did nothing — ToolStatusOverlay had mouse_filter=IGNORE,
  so meta_clicked never fired. Changed to PASS.
- Dungeon complete screen appeared only after the NEXT combat: after a boss
  kill triggered harvest, end_harvest cleared pending_continue without
  flushing queued_dungeon_complete (dungeon_mode was already false). Now
  end_harvest checks the queue first.
- Sorcerer meditate in dungeon: message now shows "(HP already full)" when
  at max, so it's obvious HP isn't healing because there's nothing to heal.

Server correctly stacked N potions into one slot with quantity=N, but the

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-04-21]  v0.9.185: Examine popup item detail reset — actually works now
v0.9.184 tried to snapshot player_info_content.text after the base
popup was built, but RichTextLabel.text does not reflect append_text
content in Godot 4 (documented behavior), so the snapshot was always
empty and _show_equipment_detail_in_popup never cleared the previous
item block.

Replaced the text-snapshot approach with a data-cache approach:
show_player_info_popup now stores the incoming data Dictionary in
player_info_last_data. _show_equipment_detail_in_popup rebuilds the
base popup by calling show_player_info_popup(player_info_last_data)
before appending the fresh item details. Clean reset every click.
------------------------------------------------------------

- Right-side Players panel (was ChatPanel) now has a "Players Online"
  header Label at the top, added at runtime during
  _move_chat_into_center_panel.
- Clicking a piece of gear in the Examine player popup now clears any
  previously displayed "Item Details" block before rendering the new
  one. show_player_info_popup snapshots the base BBCode into
  player_info_base_bbcode; _show_equipment_detail_in_popup clears and
  re-appends that base before adding the item details for the fresh
  click.

[2026-04-21]  v0.9.183: Combat bar position + Chat below action bar
Two changes:

1. Mini HP/Mana bars keep their horizontal position during combat.
   Previously _update_shortcut_buttons_visibility hid the shortcut
   container entirely; HBox collapsed that 1/3 slot and the
   [right]-aligned bars (in the now-full-width left cell) docked to
   the window edge. Now for the StatusRow-parented container we hide
   only the child buttons and keep the container visible, so the cell
   still takes its 1/3 share and the bars stay aligned with the
   GameOutput right edge.

2. Chat moved below the action bar. _move_chat_into_center_panel
   runtime-reparents ChatOutput and the InputRow from the right-side
   ChatPanel into CenterPanel, so chat now fills the space under the
   action buttons. The right-side panel becomes a dedicated Players
   Online sidebar — the old Chat/Players tab bar is hidden and
   online_players_list is made visible by default.

Runtime reparent, same pattern as StatusRow — no tscn changes.

[2026-04-21]  v0.9.182: Mini bars + shortcuts sharing one row
v0.9.181 intended for both to share a StatusRow but
_create_shortcut_buttons was still adding the buttons directly to
RootContainer — result: two separate rows, bars above buttons.

Now _create_shortcut_buttons preferentially adopts StatusRow as the
parent so both controls land in the same HBox.

Row is split 2:1 between the MiniBarsLabel (stretch_ratio=2) and the
shortcut_buttons_container (stretch_ratio=1) to mirror the
GameOutput:MapPanel ratio in TopSection. Mini bars render with [right]
so they end flush against the GameOutput/MapPanel column boundary;
shortcut buttons use BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_END so they dock to the
far right edge of the window.

Both children have SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER vertical so they line up.

1. Status panel spacing: the blank line between Backpack/Area/Nearest/

   The MiniBarsLabel goes on the left with EXPAND+FILL; the existing

   SHRINK_END flag keeps the buttons on the right). No tscn changes.

The old tscn ResourceBarsOverlay node is hidden and the @onready
variable is reassigned to the new MiniBarsLabel so every existing

HP + resource render as a single left-aligned line now (no more

justify against the shortcut buttons).

Fills the fullscreen dead space in the map panel by moving the

to between Tools and Minimap inside BottomRow.

Done at runtime in _ready() (after scene load) instead of editing the

repeatedly caused blank-screen breaks. The shortcut_buttons_container
uses the same runtime add_child technique and has always worked.

so the expansion lands on StatusHUD in the middle.

[2026-04-21]  v0.9.174: Disable autowrap on MinimapDisplay
Removing EXPAND from MinimapDisplay in v0.9.173 left the node with
min-size of zero plus default autowrap=WORD, which collapsed the
41-char-wide minimap into a single-character-wide vertical column —
tall narrow green strip filling the entire map panel.

Setting autowrap_mode = 0 (AUTOWRAP_OFF) makes the node's minimum
width equal to the widest line's natural width (~370px at font 9),
so the node fits the minimap content properly.

Single attribute addition to MinimapDisplay. No new nodes.
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-04-21]  v0.9.173: Tighten minimap frame via attribute-only changes
Removed size_flags_horizontal=3 (EXPAND+FILL) from MinimapDisplay so
the node shrinks to its fit_content width — the green frame now wraps
just the minimap instead of the full-width BottomRow cell.

Reduced StyleBoxFlat_minimap_frame corner_radius from 80 to 14 and
bumped content_margin from 6 to 10-12px so the rounded corners don't
clip the content.

No new Control children added to BottomRow — just attribute edits on
the existing node plus sub_resource tweaks.

[2026-04-21]  v0.9.162: Resource respawns near trading posts + post layout variety
Near-post resource respawns:
- Gathering nodes within 12 tiles of an NPC post now respawn after
  POST_NODE_RESPAWN_TIME (15 min) instead of being permanently depleted.
  Prevents beginner-zone nodes from being permanently farmed out.
- On server startup, existing permanent depletions near posts are
  one-time migrated to timed respawns so existing worlds benefit too.

Post layout variety:
- Trading post station groups (forge/market/quest board/etc.) are now
  placed in a per-post deterministic shuffled order based on post coords.
  Each post has a consistent layout, but posts differ from each other.
- Pairs of same-type stations (e.g., two forges) still stay adjacent to
  preserve themed clusters.
- Shuffle applies to newly generated posts; existing posts keep their
  stamped layout until regenerated.

New Status HUD panel below the minimap with at-a-glance info:
- Backpack X/40 (colored warning at 75% and 90%)
- Area Lv ~N with hotspot flag, or Safe Zone
- Compass to nearest NPC post (name + direction + distance)
- Materials pouch total count
- Top 3 active quests with progress
- Incubating egg slots — all capacity slots rendered, filled or empty;
  click an egg indicator to toggle freeze without opening a menu

Server-side: location message now includes area_level, area_is_hotspot,
area_is_safe, and nearest_post (name/direction/distance) so the client
can render these without parsing the map text.

Tutorial is now opt-in: on new character creation the player sees
"Tutorial / Skip" prompt instead of the tutorial auto-starting. If the
"disable tutorial" setting is on, no prompt is shown either.

[2026-04-21]  v0.9.159: Hotspots on safe terrain no longer trigger DANGER ZONE
The map render hides hotspot markers on safe terrain (roads, trading
posts, safe zones) — but get_hotspot_at did not apply the same check,
so stepping onto a hotspot that overlapped safe terrain fired the
"DANGER ZONE" warning even though no ! marker was visible on the map.

Also, get_monster_level_range's safe-zone early return omitted
base_level, producing the "Estimated monster level: ~0" in that
warning.

Fix: apply the same "not info.safe and not is_safe_zone" check in
get_hotspot_at, and include base_level: 0 in the safe-zone range.

Reported at ~(38 NE of origin) with no visible hotspot marker.
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-04-21]  v0.9.158: Tool durability visibility + auto-equip on break
- Persistent tool status overlay in the bottom-left of GameOutput shows
  each equipped gathering tool and its durability at all times. Color
  coded green/yellow/red.
- Gathering Complete summary now includes the tool's remaining
  durability so the player can plan replacement.
- Tool break fires a new tool_broken message type instead of a plain
  text line: renders a highlighted ◆◆◆ banner, plays the danger sound,
  and refreshes the overlay.
- Broken tool is auto-replaced by the highest-tier intact spare of the
  same subtype from the backpack, if one exists.

Implements the user's request to make tool durability visible without
opening inventory and to make tool breaks obvious.
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-04-21]  v0.9.157: Fix daily quest name mismatch on accept
Accepting daily quest [N] at a Trading Post showed one name on the
board but the server returned a different name in "Quest 'X' accepted!".

Root cause: RNG state advancement diverged between two call paths.

get_available_quests_for_player (board display) ran:
  for i in range(quest_count):
    if in active/completed/cooldown: continue  <- SKIPS before gen, RNG doesn't advance
    quest = _generate_daily_quest(... rng ...)  <- only advances for non-skipped

_regenerate_daily_quest (quest_accept lookup) ran:
  for i in range(index+1):
    quest = _generate_daily_quest(... rng ...)  <- advances for every i

So if the player had completed daily #0 and #1 earlier today, then
display generated #2 with RNG-state-after-2-skipped-iterations, while
regeneration generated #2 with RNG-state-after-2-full-iterations.
Same quest_id resolved to different data. Player saw "Conquer the
Goblin Caves" but server accepted "Journey to Northeast Farm".

Fix: always call _generate_daily_quest in the display path so the
rng advances identically in both paths; apply the completed/active/
cooldown filter AFTER generation.

Reported with reproducible screenshots (Q1-Q3).
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-04-21]  v0.9.156: Permanent fix for action-bar → item-select hotkey cascade
When an action bar slot fires on a number key that's also an item-select
key (keys 1-5 = slots 5-9 = item_1..item_5 by default), the handler only
set its own hotkey_N_pressed flag. The shared item-select key stayed free,
so any menu opened by that action immediately processed the still-held key
as a selection.

Now, when trigger_action fires on a key that matches any item-select
keycode, _consume_item_select_key locks that key in key_hold_lock until
it is physically released. No menu downstream of the action will consume
the same press.

Fixes: pressing 1 in More menu to open Quests no longer immediately
abandons Quest #1 in the quest log.
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-04-21]  v0.9.155: Quest access from More menu + /quests command
Players can't reach Quests via the R action bar slot when standing near a
gathering node (water, ore, forest) — the context-sensitive slot is hijacked
by Fish/Mine/Chop. Add two always-available fallbacks so Quests aren't gated
on terrain:

- Quests button in the More menu (slot 5)
- /quests chat command

[2026-04-15]  v0.9.147: Fix crafting XP awarded on rejected/refunded recipes
Crafting XP (skill, character, job) was being awarded before the match
statement ran, so failed attempts (upgrade at cap, enchant at cap, no

actually succeeded.

  progress messages.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-02-22]  v0.9.142: Network optimization — delta updates, gzip compression, broadcast throttling
Phase 1: Delta character updates — only changed fields sent (~60-85% reduction).
Per-frame batching coalesces multiple updates into one send. Full update forced
every 60s as desync safety net.

Phase 2: Binary framing with gzip compression for messages >512 bytes (~60-80%
size reduction). Client auto-detects legacy vs binary format for revert safety.

Phase 3: Nearby player map broadcasts batched every 300ms instead of per-move.

All three phases independently revertable via feature flags (USE_DELTA_UPDATES,
USE_COMPRESSION, USE_BROADCAST_THROTTLE) at top of server.gd.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-02-07]  v0.9.79: Fix sounds not playing - use Godot import system instead of raw FileAccess
The _load_wav_runtime() function read raw WAV bytes via FileAccess, which
fails in exported builds where WAV files are converted to .sample format
inside the PCK. Switch to load() which works with Godot's import system
in both editor and exported builds.

[2026-02-06]  v0.9.70: Combat balance overhaul - all 9 classes viable
Major balance pass with combat simulator validation (6.9% spread, 89-96% win rates).

Mage changes:
- Base mana regen: 2%/round (Sage 3%)
- Forcefield: INT×8 shield value
- Meteor cost: 12% → 8%, Blast/Meteor INT scaling: 0.03 → 0.04
- Sorcerer backfire capped at 15% max HP

Trickster changes:
- Outsmart cap: monster INT/2 → INT/3
- DEX dodge cap: 20% → 30%
- WITS-based dodge for tricksters: min(15, WITS/50)
- Monster intelligence reduced at tiers 5-9

General:
- Enrage capped at 10 stacks
- Monster HP scaling uses logarithmic equipment estimation
- Combat simulator overhauled with gauntlet/flock testing

[2026-02-05]  v0.9.68: Balance changes, blacksmith upgrades, gambling improvements
Balance & Progression:
- Early game regen doubled (levels 1-24, scales to normal by 25)
- Home Stones drop 1 tier earlier (now Tier 4-6)
- Weapon Master variants: 50% drop chance, attack-biased gear
- Shield Guardian variants: 50% drop chance, HP/defense-biased gear

Blacksmith System:
- Blacksmiths can now upgrade existing affixes on gear
- Costs gold, gems, and salvage essence (scales with item level)
- High-end gold sink for endgame progression

Gambling Improvements:
- Reduced house edge from +2 to +1
- Rolling any pair gives +1 bonus (~42% chance)
- 10% chance to win a bonus item on any winning roll

House System (Plan Updated):
- Added new upgrades: HP bonus, resource max, resource regen
- Added stat training upgrades (STR/CON/DEX/INT/WIS/WITS)

[2026-02-05]  Update changelog for v0.9.65
Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
------------------------------------------------------------
[2026-02-05]  Add Sanctuary (Roguelite House System) - v0.9.64
Major new feature: Account-level persistent house that survives permadeath

Sanctuary Features:
- ASCII map display showing kennel, storage chest, and upgrade forge
- Storage system for saving items between characters via Home Stones
- Companion Kennel for registering companions that survive death
- Baddie Points meta-currency earned when characters die
- Upgrades purchasable with BP: storage slots, kennel slots, flee chance,
  starting gold, XP bonus, gathering bonus

Home Stone Items:
- Home Stone (Equipment) - sends equipped item to storage
- Home Stone (Supplies) - sends consumables to storage
- Home Stone (Egg) - auto-hatches egg and stores companion
- Home Stone (Companion) - registers active companion to kennel

House Management:
- Discard items from storage
- Unregister companions (move from kennel to storage)
- Register companions from storage to kennel
- Withdraw items when creating new character
- Checkout registered companions for new characters

Additional Features:
- Egg freezing to pause hatching progress
- Companion sorting (level, tier, variant, damage, name, type)
- Companion/egg trading between players
